# 分类题库 22：各校专属问答（港理工、港城大、港城大东莞、澳门大学、澳门科技大学）

> **一句话定位**：这是 11 个申请项目里后 7 个项目的“该项目专属”题库：港理工 MSc Intelligent Robotics Engineering（Q1–Q25）、港城大 MSc Computer Science P53（Q26–Q50）、港城大东莞 MSc AI（Q51–Q75）、港城大东莞 MSc CS（Q76–Q100）、澳大 MSc Robotics and Autonomous Systems（Q101–Q125）、澳大 MSc Artificial Intelligence（Q126–Q150）、澳科大 MSc AI（Q151–Q175），共 175 题。前 4 个项目（港中深两个、港中文、港科广）见 [21 号文件](./21_题库_各校专属问答_港科大系与港中深港中文.md)。

**本章学完你能……**

- 针对这 7 个项目，各自说出“为什么是它”的 3 个有依据的理由，并分清哪些信息是官网已核对的、哪些要在面试前再核对；
- 在每个项目最可能的面试形式（技术面谈、短面谈、录制式视频面试、澳科大式 15–25 分钟 Zoom 面谈）下，回答专属题；
- 对每个项目最可能考的技术点（机器人控制与机电、软件工程与网络、AI 系统、估计与 SLAM、机器学习数学、IT 常识）有一版 90–120 词的英文回答；
- 在被问到“为什么同时申请 AI 和 CS（同一校区）”“为什么同时申请澳大 RAS 和 AI”“为什么选澳门”“是否申请了其他学校”时，答得诚实、清晰、不拆台。

**前置知识**：[港硕面试真题调研](../00_港硕面试真题调研.md)、[各校面试准备](../02_各校面试准备/README.md)、[CV 事实基线](../../06_维护说明/CV事实基线.md)、[PS 与推荐信主线](../../00_申请画像/02_PS与推荐信主线.md)、[目标项目与面试画像总表](../../00_申请画像/05_目标项目与面试画像总表.md)。

## 目录

1. 本文件怎么用
2. 答题通用规则
3. 第一部分：港理工 MSc Intelligent Robotics Engineering（Q1–Q25）
4. 第二部分：港城大 MSc Computer Science P53（Q26–Q50）
5. 第三部分：港城大东莞 MSc Artificial Intelligence（Q51–Q75）
6. 第四部分：港城大东莞 MSc Computer Science（Q76–Q100）
7. 第五部分：澳大 MSc Robotics and Autonomous Systems（Q101–Q125）
8. 第六部分：澳大 MSc Artificial Intelligence（Q126–Q150）
9. 第七部分：澳科大 MSc Artificial Intelligence（Q151–Q175）
10. 自测与练习安排

## 1. 本文件怎么用

1. **按你被邀请面试的项目找到对应部分**。每部分开头有“项目一页要点”“面试形式与证据”“面试前核对清单”，请先读。
2. **每个项目 25 题**，分五类：A 项目匹配度与动机，B 课程与研究方向，C 该校面试形式可能出现的题，D 技术考点预测，E 项目深挖与收尾。
3. **英文回答 85–120 词**，是示范，不是台词。先读、再合上书用自己的话说。
4. **官方信息的表述规则**：凡课程名、学分、学费、面试形式不能 100% 确认的内容，回答里一律使用 “From the programme page, my understanding is …”、“As far as I know …”、“I will check the official page before the interview.”；每部分的核对清单写明要核对什么。**不编造课程名、教授名、研究组名**。港城大东莞与澳科大两个项目的课程名单在本仓库材料里没有，回答里只说培养目标和方向词。
5. **事实规则**：项目数字取自各项目 README 的实机细节自查表；用 about / roughly；不记得就用诚实模板。P1 的 LQR 是分析工具而非部署；P3 控制任务约 3 ms，不说 1 kHz；3V3 二等奖和全国赛三等奖是团队成绩；P5 用 ROS2 Humble；P7 是经典 CAN；P6 的 IMU 为 100 Hz；P4 是 YOLOv5/RKNN INT8；读码发现的隐患不是你的贡献。
6. **与其他题库的关系**：通用动机见 [02 题库](./02_题库_为什么这个项目_学校_地区.md)；AI 视频面试通用题见 [20](./20_题库_AI视频面试与限时作答.md)；控制、估计、规划、ML、CS 基础的技术深挖见 [11](./11_题库_控制理论与机器人控制.md)、[12](./12_题库_运动学_动力学_状态估计_SLAM.md)、[13](./13_题库_规划_导航_感知.md)、[14](./14_题库_机器学习_深度学习_强化学习_大模型.md)、[15](./15_题库_嵌入式_RTOS_通信_软件_CS基础.md)。

## 2. 答题通用规则

| 情况 | 怎么说 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 课程名没有 100% 把握 | 只说方向词，加“我了解到” | “From the programme page, my understanding is that the core includes motion planning.” |
| 面试形式不确定 | 不预设，说准备了几种 | “I have prepared for both a short conversation and a recorded format.” |
| 被问学校排名/比较 | 不比较，只谈匹配 | “I focus on how the programme fits my gap.” |
| 技术题不会 | 先说边界，再推理，再确认 | “I haven’t used it directly, but from the principle I would expect … Is that right?” |
| 数字记不清 | 诚实模板 | “I don’t remember the exact number, but roughly …” |
| 被问是否申请了其他学校 | 诚实、不排名 | “Yes, I applied to several programmes; this one fits my plan well.” |
| 同校两个项目都申请 | 讲清需求差异 | “They answer two different needs: …” |

> **面试要点**：评委不会因为你“知道很多课程名”给高分，但会因为你编造信息扣分。知道的说清，不确定的明说“我会再核对”。

---

## 3. 第一部分：港理工 MSc in Intelligent Robotics Engineering（Q1–Q25）

### 3.1 项目一页要点与面试形式

| 项 | 内容（来源与等级） |
|---|---|
| 项目 | MSc in Intelligent Robotics Engineering，香港理工大学；全日制 1.5 年；31 学分（官方核对表）；所属院系你的 PS 写的是 Department of Mechanical Engineering，**面试前以官网核对** |
| 核心课程（官网所列方向） | Embodied Robot Intelligence、Principles of Robotic Mechanisms、Robot Motion Planning、Soft Robotics、Advanced Control Technology、Advanced Product Mechatronics；选修方向含自动驾驶、计算机视觉与图像处理、高级 AI、人机系统等（**选修开设情况以官网为准**） |
| 其他 | 课程通常在工作日晚上；有 dissertation 路线（PS 的意向）；官方：合适申请人**可能**被邀请面试或测试，形式未公布 |
| 与你的匹配 | 最对口：P1/P7 控制与闭链运动学、P5 规划与跟踪、P2 操作与重力补偿；PS 主线“从调参到模型” |
| 风险 | 机构学（mechanisms）与软体机器人是你的缺口；CS 背景被质疑工程深度；不要把 LQR 说成 P1 的部署控制器 |

**面试前核对清单**：① 所属院系与论文路线要求；② 选修课真实名单；③ 面试 / 测试的形式、是否有笔试；④ 早轮 / 主轮日期以系统记录为准。

**A. 项目匹配度与动机（Q1–Q5）**

### Q1. Why Intelligent Robotics Engineering at PolyU, and not a computer science or AI programme?

**中文题意**：为什么选港理工智能机器人工程，而不是计算机或 AI 项目？
**考察点**：专业选择的逻辑。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：Because my questions are now about physical systems: why a controller works, how a mechanism limits it, and how sensing limits it. From the programme page, my understanding is that this programme combines control, motion planning, mechanisms and embodied intelligence in one degree, with an engineering focus. A computer science programme would deepen my software, which I value, but I already have a computer science degree. What I lack is exactly the engineering theory: dynamics, advanced control and mechanisms. I think my software background is an advantage here, because I can implement and test quickly. What I need is the theory that tells me what to implement.
**中文翻译**：因为我现在的问题关乎物理系统：控制器为什么有效、机构如何限制它、传感如何限制它。就我从项目页面了解的，这个项目把控制、运动规划、机构和具身智能放在一个有工程导向的学位里。计算机项目会加深我的软件能力，我也看重，但我已有计算机学位。我缺的正是工程理论：动力学、先进控制和机构。我认为软件背景在这里是优势，因为我能快速实现和测试。我需要的是告诉我该实现什么的理论。
**追问 1**：Do you have a mechanical background? — Not formally; I have worked with the consequences of mechanical design, such as linkage kinematics and torque limits. *（中文：有机械背景吗？— 没有正式的；我处理过机械设计带来的后果，如连杆运动学和力矩限制。）*
**追问 2**：What would you do for the gap? — Review statics and kinematics before arrival, and learn from mechanical classmates. *（中文：如何补缺口？— 入学前复习静力学和运动学，并向机械背景的同学学习。）*
**关键词与易错点**：engineering theory、gap；不要贬低计算机项目；诚实说机械背景。

### Q2. The programme is hosted by an engineering department. How does your computer science training help you there?

**中文题意**：项目由工程系开设，你的计算机训练在那里有什么帮助？
**考察点**：背景互补。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：Modern robots are software-heavy, and my training helps in three ways. First, I write real-time code in C and C++ on microcontrollers and Linux, which is needed to put a controller on a robot. Second, I know operating systems and concurrency, which matter for timing and for communication over CAN or middleware. Third, I can set up tests, logging and automation, so experiments are repeatable. I also know the limits: I do not have the mechanical design background of engineering students. I would learn from them, and I would help them with software and testing in group projects. I think that is a fair exchange.
**中文翻译**：现代机器人高度依赖软件，我的训练在三方面有帮助。第一，我在微控制器和 Linux 上写实时 C/C++ 代码，这是把控制器放到机器人上所需要的。第二，我懂操作系统和并发，这对时序和 CAN 或中间件通信很重要。第三，我能搭建测试、日志和自动化，使实验可重复。我也知道局限：我没有工程专业学生的机械设计背景。我会向他们学习，并在小组项目中帮助他们做软件和测试。我认为这是公平的交换。
**追问 1**：Give an example of automation. — A script that replays a test and plots the key signals, which cut a regression from about fifteen minutes to one. *（中文：举一个自动化例子。— 一个重放测试并画关键信号的脚本，把回归从约十五分钟缩到一分钟。）*
**追问 2**：Will you be at a disadvantage in mechanics? — In the beginning yes, so I will start early. *（中文：机械上会不利吗？— 一开始会，所以我会提前开始。）*
**关键词与易错点**：complementary、fair exchange；P1 回归脚本取自自查表；不要说“CS is better than ME”。

### Q3. Classes are usually in the evening on weekdays. How will you manage this?

**中文题意**：课程通常在工作日晚上，你怎么安排？
**考察点**：对项目安排的了解和务实。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：From the programme information, my understanding is that classes are mainly on weekday evenings, and I will confirm the timetable on the official page. For me, it is practical because I can use the daytime for self-study, lab work and projects, and I can attend internships or part-time project work if it is allowed and does not conflict with the study. I am used to a full schedule. I would also plan my travel and rest, so that I do not arrive tired in the evening classes. If there are daytime or weekend sessions, I will adapt.
**中文翻译**：就我从项目信息了解的，课程主要在工作日晚上，我会在官网确认时间表。这对我是务实的，因为白天可以自学、做实验室工作和项目，如果允许且不与学业冲突，也可以参加实习或兼职项目。我习惯满档的日程。我也会规划通勤与休息，避免晚上上课时太累。如果有白天或周末的课，我会适应。
**追问 1**：Will you work while studying? — Only if it is allowed and does not affect my grades. *（中文：会边学边工作吗？— 仅在允许且不影响成绩时。）*
**追问 2**：What do you do in the daytime? — Self-study, lab-style projects and preparation for the dissertation. *（中文：白天做什么？— 自学、类实验室项目和论文准备。）*
**关键词与易错点**：confirm timetable、practical；不要承诺兼职；不要抱怨晚课。

### Q4. Would you choose the dissertation route or the coursework route, and why?

**中文题意**：你会选论文路线还是纯课程路线，为什么？
**考察点**：学术意向（PS 写了论文路线）。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I intend to take the dissertation route, if the programme allows it. In my projects, I worked mainly by tuning on the robot and reasoning afterwards. A dissertation would give me a structured research experience that I have not had: defining a question, setting metrics, comparing against a baseline, and writing it up properly. My topic could be balance control under changing configuration, using what I know from the ballbot and the wheel-legged robot. I will check the requirements of the dissertation route on the official page, for example the credits and any quota, and I will discuss the scope with a supervisor.
**中文翻译**：如果项目允许，我打算走论文路线。在我的项目里，我主要是在机器人上调参，事后再推理。论文能给我尚未有过的结构化研究经历：定义问题、设定指标、与基线比较并认真写成文。题目可以是构型变化下的平衡控制，利用我在球形机器人和轮腿机器人上的经验。我会在官网核对论文路线的要求，如学分和名额，并与导师讨论范围。
**追问 1**：What if the dissertation route is full? — I would do the coursework route and still seek a project. *（中文：论文路线满了呢？— 我会走课程路线，仍寻找项目。）*
**追问 2**：What is your first metric? — Settling time after a push, over repeated trials. *（中文：第一个指标？— 受推后的稳定时间，多次重复。）*
**关键词与易错点**：structured research、baseline、metrics；名额和学分以官网为准。

### Q5. Why Hong Kong, and why PolyU for the Greater Bay Area career you want?

**中文题意**：为什么选香港，为什么港理工适合你的大湾区职业？
**考察点**：地域与职业的逻辑。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I plan to work in the Greater Bay Area as a robotics algorithm engineer, and Hong Kong is close to Shenzhen, where I built my experience and where I have contacts. As far as I know, PolyU has a strong applied and engineering orientation, which fits a programme about robotic systems. The evening schedule also makes it easier to connect study with real projects. I do not want to overstate my knowledge of the university. If I get an offer, I will visit, meet students and ask about projects with industry. My goal is to be useful in the region after graduation.
**中文翻译**：我计划在大湾区做机器人算法工程师，香港离深圳近，我在深圳积累了经验也有人脉。据我所知，港理工有较强的应用和工程导向，适合以机器人系统为主题的项目。晚间课程也让学习与真实项目结合更容易。我不想夸大对学校的了解。如果拿到 offer，我会去参观、认识同学，并询问与产业合作的项目。我的目标是毕业后在这一地区做出贡献。
**追问 1**：Which industry links interest you? — Robot companies and teams building autonomy software. *（中文：对哪些产业联系感兴趣？— 机器人公司和做自主软件的团队。）*
**追问 2**：Have you visited campus? — Not yet. *（中文：去过校园吗？— 还没有。）*
**关键词与易错点**：applied orientation（as far as I know）；不夸大；没去过就说没去过。

**B. 课程与研究方向（Q6–Q11）**

### Q6. What do you hope to learn in Embodied Robot Intelligence?

**中文题意**：你希望在“具身机器人智能”里学到什么？
**考察点**：与核心课程的对应。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：From the programme page, my understanding is that this course area is about intelligence that works through a robot body, connecting perception, decision and action. I hope to learn how learning-based methods fit with control and planning: how a policy is trained, how perception feeds control, and how to evaluate it on a robot. My background is the control and systems side. I have also built a vision-to-decision-to-action loop in a small gardening system, and a safety-checked execution chain at Kaihong. I want to build the learning foundations that I lack, and to understand where learning helps and where classical control is better.
**中文翻译**：就我从项目页面了解的，这个课程领域关于通过机器人身体运作的智能，把感知、决策和动作连起来。我希望学到基于学习的方法如何与控制和规划结合：策略怎么训练、感知如何喂给控制、如何在机器人上评估。我的背景是控制与系统一侧。我也在一个小型园艺系统里做过从视觉到决策到动作的回路，并在深开鸿做过带安全检查的执行链。我想补上缺少的学习基础，并理解学习在哪有帮助、经典控制在哪更好。
**追问 1**：Is your gardening system robot learning? — No; it is an embodied loop, but it does not learn a policy. *（中文：园艺系统是机器人学习吗？— 不是，它是具身回路，但不学习策略。）*
**追问 2**：Where is classical control better? — Low-level stability, safety guarantees and predictable timing. *（中文：经典控制在哪更好？— 底层稳定性、安全保证和可预测的时序。）*
**关键词与易错点**：perception–decision–action；不把园艺项目说成机器人学习；课程内容用“我了解到”。

### Q7. What interests you in Principles of Robotic Mechanisms?

**中文题意**：你对“机器人机构原理”有什么兴趣？
**考察点**：机构学缺口的诚实回答。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I have implemented closed-chain kinematics for a wheel-legged robot, with a five-bar leg, but only for one robot and mostly from the computational side. I want to learn the general framework: degrees of freedom of mechanisms, the types of joints, linkages, constraints, singularities and workspace, and how mechanism design affects control. For example, I would like to understand why a design is chosen, and not just how to compute its kinematics. I also hope to see how different actuators and transmissions limit torque and speed. This would help me when I tune controllers, because I would know what the hardware can really do.
**中文翻译**：我为轮腿机器人实现过闭链运动学，腿是五连杆，但只针对一台机器人，且主要从计算侧。我想学习一般性的框架：机构的自由度、关节类型、连杆、约束、奇异性与工作空间，以及机构设计如何影响控制。例如，我想理解为什么选择某种设计，而不只是如何计算它的运动学。我也希望看到不同的执行器和传动如何限制力矩与速度。这会帮助我调控制器，因为我会知道硬件真正能做什么。
**追问 1**：How many degrees of freedom does a planar five-bar linkage have? — Two. *（中文：平面五连杆有几个自由度？— 两个。）*
**追问 2**：What is a singularity of a mechanism? — A configuration where it loses the ability to move or resist force in some direction. *（中文：机构奇异位形是什么？— 在某方向失去运动或承力能力的位形。）*
**关键词与易错点**：DOF、constraints、singularities；承认只做过计算侧；不说设计过机构。

### Q8. What do you expect from Robot Motion Planning?

**中文题意**：你对“机器人运动规划”有什么期待？
**考察点**：与 P5 的衔接和理论深度。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I have built a planning chain for a car-like robot: Hybrid-A* for the global path, TEB for local trajectories, and MPC tracking. I know how to configure and tune them, but I want the theory: configuration space, completeness and optimality of search-based and sampling-based planners, trajectory optimisation, and how to plan with dynamics and constraints. I would also like to learn about planning for manipulators, which I have not done. A concrete question I want to answer is why the planner and the controller sometimes disagree, for example when the reference jumps between homotopy classes, which I saw in my tuning.
**中文翻译**：我为车式机器人做过规划链：Hybrid-A* 做全局路径，TEB 做局部轨迹，MPC 做跟踪。我会配置和调参，但我想学理论：构型空间、基于搜索和基于采样的规划器的完备性与最优性、轨迹优化，以及如何带动力学和约束做规划。我还想学习机械臂的规划，这是我没做过的。一个我想回答的具体问题是：规划器与控制器为什么有时不一致，例如参考在同伦类之间跳变，这是我在调参时见到的。
**追问 1**：What is configuration space? — The space of all robot configurations, where obstacles become forbidden regions. *（中文：什么是构型空间？— 机器人所有构型的空间，障碍变成禁区。）*
**追问 2**：Have you done manipulator planning? — Only inverse kinematics tracking, not collision-free planning. *（中文：做过机械臂规划吗？— 只做过逆运动学跟踪，没做无碰撞规划。）*
**关键词与易错点**：completeness、optimality、homotopy；TEB 同伦类切换见 P5 自查表；不夸大。

### Q9. You said you do not know soft robotics. Why does it attract you at all?

**中文题意**：你说不了解软体机器人，那它为什么还吸引你？
**考察点**：对陌生领域的态度。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I know it at a general level: soft robots use compliant materials, so they are safe for interaction and can adapt their shape, but they are hard to model and control because of many degrees of freedom and nonlinear materials. It attracts me because it challenges my usual approach. My robots are rigid, with clear models. Soft robots force you to think about modelling under uncertainty, sensing and control, which are skills I want to strengthen. I have no hands-on experience, and I do not want to pretend. I would approach it from what I know: modelling, sensing and feedback.
**中文翻译**：我只有概括性的了解：软体机器人使用柔顺材料，所以交互安全、形状可适应，但由于自由度多、材料非线性，建模和控制困难。它吸引我，是因为它挑战我惯用的方法。我的机器人是刚性的，有清晰的模型。软体机器人迫使你思考不确定性下的建模、传感与控制，这些都是我想加强的能力。我没有动手经验，也不想假装有。我会从已知出发：建模、传感和反馈。
**追问 1**：What is hard to control in soft robots? — Their state is high-dimensional and hard to measure. *（中文：软体机器人难控制在哪？— 状态高维且难测量。）*
**追问 2**：Would you work on it for the dissertation? — Possibly, if I can find a well-defined question with a supervisor. *（中文：会做成论文吗？— 如果能与导师找到明确的问题，有可能。）*
**关键词与易错点**：compliant、nonlinear、honest；不假装有经验。

### Q10. What do you want from Advanced Control Technology?

**中文题意**：你想从“先进控制技术”里得到什么？
**考察点**：核心控制理论缺口。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：I have used cascaded PID, gain-scheduled LQR, sliding mode and MPC, but mostly by implementing and tuning on robots. I want the theory that explains them: stability analysis, robustness, frequency-domain thinking such as bandwidth and margins, optimal control and its connection to Riccati equations, model predictive control with guarantees, and system identification. For example, on the ballbot, an LQR analysis helped me read my PID gains, and I want to extend that into a systematic design method. I also want to know when a more advanced method is worth the cost, since many robots work well with simple controllers.
**中文翻译**：我用过级联 PID、增益调度 LQR、滑模和 MPC，但主要是在机器人上实现和调参。我想学解释它们的理论：稳定性分析、鲁棒性、带宽与裕度这样的频域思维、最优控制及其与 Riccati 方程的联系、带保证的模型预测控制，以及系统辨识。例如在球形机器人上，LQR 分析帮我解读 PID 增益，我想把它发展成系统的设计方法。我也想知道什么时候更先进的方法值得付出代价，因为许多机器人用简单控制器也工作得很好。
**追问 1**：What is a phase margin? — How much extra phase lag the loop can take before becoming unstable. *（中文：相位裕度是什么？— 回路在失稳前能再承受多少相位滞后。）*
**追问 2**：Did you deploy LQR on the ballbot? — No, I used it for analysis; I deployed the PID cascade. *（中文：球形机器人上部署了 LQR 吗？— 没有，用于分析；部署的是 PID 级联。）*
**关键词与易错点**：stability、robustness、margins；P1 LQR 为分析；P7 部署。

### Q11. What does mechatronics mean to you, and what is your experience?

**中文题意**：机电一体化对你意味着什么？你有什么经验？
**考察点**：与 Advanced Product Mechatronics 的联系。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：For me, mechatronics means designing the mechanics, electronics, sensing and software together, so the system works as a whole. My experience is on the electronics and software side. On the RoboMaster hero robot, I programmed an STM32F407 with FreeRTOS to control motors over CAN and read an IMU through SPI. On the wheel-legged robot, I used an STM32H7 with FDCAN for torque commands. I learned about motor limits, encoder feedback, bus load and power. I did not design the mechanics or the power electronics myself. I want to learn product-level design: how requirements, mechanics, electronics and software trade off in a real product.
**中文翻译**：对我来说，机电一体化意味着把机械、电子、传感和软件一起设计，使系统作为整体工作。我的经验在电子和软件一侧。在 RoboMaster 英雄机器人上，我给 STM32F407 加 FreeRTOS 编程，通过 CAN 控制电机，通过 SPI 读取 IMU。在轮腿机器人上，我用 STM32H7 加 FDCAN 发送力矩命令。我了解了电机限制、编码器反馈、总线负载和功率。机械和功率电子不是我设计的。我想学习产品级设计：需求、机械、电子和软件如何在真实产品中权衡。
**追问 1**：What is CAN bus load and why does it matter? — The fraction of bus time used by frames; too high causes delays or lost messages. *（中文：CAN 总线负载是什么？为什么重要？— 帧占用总线时间的比例，过高会造成延迟或丢包。）*
**追问 2**：What did you design in hardware? — Nothing; I worked with the platforms provided. *（中文：硬件上设计了什么？— 没有，我使用提供的平台。）*
**关键词与易错点**：STM32F407、FDCAN、bus load；机械和功率电子非你所做；P7 是经典 CAN 模式。

**C. 该校面试形式可能出现的题（Q12–Q14）**

### Q12. At the whiteboard: linearise an inverted pendulum and say how you would choose Q and R.

**中文题意**：在白板上：线性化倒立摆，并说明如何选 Q 和 R。
**考察点**：技术面谈中的推导口述。
**English answer（约 110 词）**：I write the equation of motion in terms of the tilt angle. At the upright point, I use small-angle approximations, so sine theta is about theta and cosine is about one, which gives a linear state-space model with states such as position, velocity, tilt and tilt rate. For Q and R, I start with Bryson's rule: set each diagonal entry to one over the square of the maximum acceptable value of that state or input. Then I increase the weight on tilt if I need a faster correction, and increase the weight on the input if the actuator saturates. I check the closed-loop poles and test in simulation, and then on the robot with limits.
**中文翻译**：我用倾角写出运动方程。在直立点，我用小角度近似，sinθ≈θ，cosθ≈1，得到状态空间线性模型，状态如位置、速度、倾角和倾角速度。选 Q 和 R 时，我从 Bryson 法则开始：把每个对角元设为该状态或输入最大可接受值平方的倒数。然后，如果需要更快的修正就加大倾角的权重，如果执行器饱和就加大输入的权重。我检查闭环极点，先在仿真中测试，再带着限制上机。
**追问 1**：What does a bigger R do? — It penalises the control effort more, so the response is gentler. *（中文：R 变大会怎样？— 更惩罚控制量，响应更温和。）*
**追问 2**：What is the cost function? — The integral of x transpose Q x plus u transpose R u. *（中文：代价函数是什么？— x'Qx + u'Ru 的积分。）*
**关键词与易错点**：Bryson's rule、weights vs saturation；用 “in the ballbot analysis I used weights of this kind” 不报没核对的值。

### Q13. Explain your balance control project to a non-specialist panel member.

**中文题意**：向非专业的评委解释你的平衡控制项目。
**考察点**：沟通清晰度。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：Imagine balancing a broom on your palm. You watch how it leans and move your hand under it. My robot does the same on three small wheels sitting on a ball. Sensors measure how much it leans and how fast. A controller decides how to turn the wheels. It works in two layers: an outer layer decides how much to lean to move to a place, and an inner layer keeps the lean under control. The robot also has two arms. When the arms move, the balance point moves, so I calculated that shift and added a correction. The result is a robot that stays balanced while its arms move.
**中文翻译**：想象在手掌上平衡一把扫帚。你看它倾斜多少，并把手移到它下面。我的机器人在球上的三个小轮子上做同样的事。传感器测量它倾斜多少、多快。控制器决定如何转动轮子。它分两层：外层决定为了移动到某处应倾斜多少，内层把倾斜控制住。机器人还有两只手臂。手臂移动时平衡点也会移动，所以我计算这个偏移并加了修正。结果是手臂移动时机器人仍保持平衡。
**追问 1**：Why two layers? — The inner one reacts quickly, and the outer one can be slow and simple. *（中文：为什么两层？— 内层反应快，外层可以慢而简单。）*
**追问 2**：How did you know the correction worked? — I switched it on and off with the same arm motion and compared. *（中文：怎么知道修正有效？— 用同一手臂运动开关它并比较。）*
**关键词与易错点**：broom analogy、two layers；少术语；不要报没核对的数字。

### Q14. Pitch your dissertation idea in one minute.

**中文题意**：用一分钟推介你的论文设想。
**考察点**：研究设想的清晰度。
**English answer（约 110 词）**：My idea is balance control under changing configuration. When the centre of mass shifts or the leg length changes, a fixed controller loses performance. I would compare three designs on one platform in simulation first, then on a lab robot: a fixed-gain controller, a gain-scheduled controller, and the same controller with a learned or estimated correction term. The metrics would be recovery from a push, tracking error and control effort, over repeated trials. I would build on my experience with gain-scheduled LQR and centre-of-mass feedforward. I know the idea is early, and I would refine it with a supervisor and keep the scope small enough to finish properly.
**中文翻译**：我的设想是构型变化下的平衡控制。当质心移动或腿长变化时，固定的控制器性能下降。我会在同一平台上比较三种设计，先仿真再用实验室机器人：固定增益控制器、增益调度控制器，以及加入学习或估计修正项的同一控制器。指标是受推后的恢复、跟踪误差和控制量，多次重复试验。我会基于我在增益调度 LQR 和质心前馈上的经验。我知道想法还早，会与导师细化，并把范围保持得足够小，以便做完。
**追问 1**：What is your hypothesis? — Scheduling or correction improves recovery over a fixed gain in the changed configuration. *（中文：你的假设？— 在变化的构型下，调度或修正比固定增益更改善恢复。）*
**追问 2**：What if it does not? — I report it honestly and analyse why. *（中文：如果没有呢？— 如实报告并分析原因。）*
**关键词与易错点**：three designs、metrics、small scope；不声称已有结果。

**D. 技术考点预测（Q15–Q23）**

### Q15. What does it mean for a system to be stable, and how do you check it?

**中文题意**：系统稳定是什么意思？如何检查？
**考察点**：控制基础。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：For a linear time-invariant system, asymptotic stability means that the state returns to equilibrium from any initial condition. We check it from the poles: all eigenvalues of the system matrix must have negative real parts for a continuous system, or magnitude less than one for a discrete one. For nonlinear systems, we use Lyapunov methods or linearise around the operating point. In practice, I also look at the response: overshoot, oscillation and settling time. A model can be stable and still saturate the actuator, so I check limits as well. For a closed loop, the controller must make all poles stable.
**中文翻译**：对线性时不变系统，渐近稳定意味着状态从任何初值回到平衡点。我们通过极点检查：连续系统要求系统矩阵所有特征值实部为负，离散系统要求模小于一。对非线性系统，用李雅普诺夫方法或在工作点附近线性化。实践中，我也看响应：超调、振荡和调节时间。模型可以稳定但仍使执行器饱和，所以我也检查限幅。对闭环，控制器必须使所有极点稳定。
**追问 1**：What about a system with delay? — Delay adds phase lag and reduces the stability margin. *（中文：有延迟的系统呢？— 延迟增加相位滞后，降低稳定裕度。）*
**追问 2**：What is Lyapunov stability in one sentence? — An energy-like function that decreases along trajectories. *（中文：一句话说李雅普诺夫稳定？— 沿轨迹下降的类能量函数。）*
**关键词与易错点**：poles、negative real part、Lyapunov；离散判据是模小于 1；不要混淆连续和离散。

### Q16. Why does an integral term remove steady-state error, and what are its risks?

**中文题意**：为什么积分项能消除稳态误差？有什么风险？
**考察点**：PID 理解。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：The integral term accumulates the error over time. If a steady error remains, the integral keeps growing and pushes the output until the error is zero. So at equilibrium, the integrator holds exactly the constant effort needed to cancel the disturbance or offset. The risks are windup, when the actuator saturates and the integral keeps growing, which causes large overshoot later, and slower, more oscillatory response if the integral gain is too large. Remedies are clamping the integral, stopping accumulation when the output is saturated, and resetting at re-enable. On the ballbot, I used a small integral with a limit, to absorb the residual of a mounting offset.
**中文翻译**：积分项随时间累积误差。如果仍有稳态误差，积分会不断增大并推动输出，直到误差为零。所以在平衡点，积分器恰好保持抵消扰动或偏置所需的恒定作用。风险是积分饱和，即执行器饱和而积分继续增大，之后造成大的超调；以及积分增益过大会使响应更慢、振荡更多。办法是积分限幅、输出饱和时停止累加，以及重新使能时复位。在球形机器人上，我用了带限幅的小积分，吸收安装偏置的残差。
**追问 1**：Is the ballbot integral large? — No; it is small with a limit. *（中文：积分大吗？— 不，小且带限幅。）*
**追问 2**：Is clamping the same as back-calculation? — No; back-calculation feeds the saturation error back to the integrator, and I used clamping. *（中文：钳位等于反算吗？— 不是；反算把饱和误差反馈给积分器，我用的是钳位。）*
**关键词与易错点**：windup、clamping；P1 用积分限幅，不是反算；不要夸大。

### Q17. What are bandwidth and phase margin, and why do they matter in cascade control?

**中文题意**：带宽和相位裕度是什么？为什么对级联控制重要？
**考察点**：频域基础（你较弱，诚实回答）。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：Bandwidth is roughly the frequency range over which the closed loop follows its reference well. Phase margin measures how far the loop is from instability: the extra phase lag at the gain crossover frequency that would make the loop unstable. A larger margin means less overshoot and more tolerance to delay. In cascade control, the inner loop should be much faster than the outer loop, typically several times faster, so the outer loop sees the inner loop as nearly ideal. On the ballbot, the inner attitude loop was around six or seven times faster than the position loop. I learned frequency-domain ideas from my control courses, but I use them mostly qualitatively.
**中文翻译**：带宽大致是闭环能良好跟随参考的频率范围。相位裕度衡量回路离失稳多远：在增益穿越频率处，再增加多少相位滞后会使回路失稳。裕度越大，超调越小，对延迟的容忍越大。在级联控制中，内环应比外环快得多，通常快数倍，这样外环看到的内环近似理想。在球形机器人上，内环姿态回路约比位置回路快六七倍。我在控制课程中学过频域思想，但主要定性地使用。
**追问 1**：Why not make the loops equally fast? — They would interact and can oscillate. *（中文：为什么不让两环一样快？— 它们会相互作用并可能振荡。）*
**追问 2**：Can you read a Bode plot? — Yes, at a basic level; I want to practise more. *（中文：能看伯德图吗？— 能，基础水平；想多练习。）*
**关键词与易错点**：time-scale separation、qualitative；带宽比约 6~7 取自 P1 自查表；诚实说明主要定性使用。

### Q18. Explain homogeneous transformations and why they are used in robotics.

**中文题意**：解释齐次变换及其在机器人中的用途。
**考察点**：运动学基础。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：A homogeneous transformation is a four by four matrix that combines a rotation and a translation, so a rigid motion becomes a single matrix multiplication. With it, I can chain transforms: the pose of the end effector in the base frame is the product of the transforms along the arm. It also makes it easy to change frames, for example from camera to robot base, or from the VR controller to the arm target. Orthogonality of the rotation part gives a simple inverse. In my projects, I also learned how important it is to define frames and conventions clearly, because many bugs came from mixing them.
**中文翻译**：齐次变换是一个 4×4 矩阵，合并了旋转和平移，使刚体运动变成一次矩阵乘法。有了它，我可以串联变换：末端在基座坐标系中的位姿是沿机械臂各变换的乘积。它也使坐标系转换很方便，例如从相机到机器人基座，或从 VR 手柄到机械臂目标。旋转部分的正交性使求逆很简单。在我的项目里，我也认识到清晰定义坐标系和约定非常重要，因为许多 bug 来自混用。
**追问 1**：How do you invert it? — Transpose the rotation and rotate-negate the translation. *（中文：怎么求逆？— 转置旋转部分，平移取负并旋转。）*
**追问 2**：Which frame bug did you meet? — Axis conventions between the VR system and the robot base. *（中文：遇到过什么坐标系 bug？— VR 系统与机器人基座之间的轴约定。）*
**关键词与易错点**：4×4、chain、inverse；帧的右手定则约定；P2 的轴映射取自自查表。

### Q19. Compare the Lagrangian and Newton–Euler approaches to robot dynamics.

**中文题意**：比较拉格朗日与牛顿–欧拉两种机器人动力学方法。
**考察点**：动力学基础。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：Both give the same equations of motion. The Newton–Euler method writes force and torque balance for each link and propagates velocities and accelerations outward and forces inward; it is efficient for computation, and many libraries use a version of it. The Lagrangian method starts from the kinetic and potential energy and derives the equations in generalised coordinates; it gives a clear structure, M(q) q double dot plus C q dot plus g equals tau, and is good for analysis and control design. In practice, I used libraries such as KDL and Pinocchio from the URDF to compute gravity terms, and a Lagrangian-style model for the inverted pendulum.
**中文翻译**：两者给出相同的运动方程。牛顿–欧拉法对每个连杆写出力与力矩平衡，向外传播速度和加速度、向内传播力；计算高效，许多库使用其变体。拉格朗日法从动能和势能出发，在广义坐标下推导方程；结构清晰，M(q)q̈ + C q̇ + g = τ，适合分析和控制设计。实践中，我用 KDL、Pinocchio 等库，从 URDF 计算重力项，并用拉格朗日风格的模型处理倒立摆。
**追问 1**：What is g(q)? — The gravity torque at configuration q. *（中文：g(q) 是什么？— 构型 q 下的重力力矩。）*
**追问 2**：Which would you use for control design? — Lagrangian structure for analysis, Newton–Euler libraries for computing. *（中文：控制设计用哪个？— 用拉格朗日结构做分析，牛顿–欧拉库做计算。）*
**关键词与易错点**：energy-based vs force-based、M C g；不要说两者结果不同。

### Q20. What is a singularity in a manipulator, and how do you handle it numerically?

**中文题意**：机械臂奇异点是什么？数值上怎么处理？
**考察点**：运动学与实践。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：A singularity is a configuration where the Jacobian loses rank, so the end effector cannot move in some direction, and the inverse kinematics asks for very large joint speeds. Typical cases are a fully stretched arm or aligned joint axes. To handle it numerically, I use damped least squares, which adds a small damping term when inverting the Jacobian, trading some tracking accuracy for bounded joint speeds. I also apply joint velocity limits, and I monitor the smallest singular value to reduce the target speed when it gets small. In my dual-arm teleoperation, this prevented joints from spinning near singular configurations.
**中文翻译**：奇异点是雅可比降秩的构型，此时末端无法在某些方向运动，逆运动学要求极大的关节速度。典型情形是手臂完全伸直或关节轴共线。数值上我用阻尼最小二乘，在求雅可比逆时加一个小阻尼项，以牺牲部分跟踪精度换取有界的关节速度。我也加关节速度限制，并监控最小奇异值，当它变小时降低目标速度。在双臂遥操作中，这避免了关节在奇异构型附近猛转。
**追问 1**：What threshold did you use for the smallest singular value? — About 0.05 for flagging and slowing down. *（中文：最小奇异值阈值？— 约 0.05 用于标记和降速。）*
**追问 2**：What is the trade-off of damping? — Larger damping is safer but less accurate. *（中文：阻尼的权衡？— 阻尼越大越安全但越不准。）*
**关键词与易错点**：rank loss、damped least squares、σmin；0.05 取自 P2 自查表，说 about。

### Q21. How does a motor current loop relate to the torque and speed loops you tuned?

**中文题意**：电机电流环与你调的力矩环和速度环有什么关系？
**考察点**：机电层次理解。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：For a brushless motor, the torque is roughly proportional to the current, so a current loop in the driver is the innermost loop and is much faster than the others. A speed loop sits outside it, and a position loop outside that, which is again a cascade. In my projects, the drivers already had the current loop, so I tuned the speed or position loops on top, or sent torque commands for the wheel-legged robot. I had to respect the torque limits and the CAN update rate. Speed estimates from encoders are noisy, so I paid attention to filtering and sampling. I did not tune the driver's current loop myself.
**中文翻译**：对无刷电机，力矩大致与电流成正比，所以驱动器中的电流环是最内层，比其他环快得多。速度环在它外面，位置环在更外面，这又是一个级联。在我的项目里，驱动器已有电流环，所以我在其上调速度或位置环，或者在轮腿机器人上直接发送力矩命令。我必须遵守力矩限制和 CAN 更新率。编码器的速度估计有噪声，所以我注意滤波和采样。驱动器的电流环不是我调的。
**追问 1**：Why is the current loop fastest? — The electrical dynamics are faster than the mechanical ones. *（中文：为什么电流环最快？— 电气动态比机械动态快。）*
**追问 2**：Which loop did you tune for the chassis wheels? — A wheel-speed PID, with encoder feedback at 200 Hz. *（中文：底盘轮子调的哪个环？— 轮速 PID，编码器反馈 200 Hz。）*
**关键词与易错点**：torque ∝ current、cascade；轮速 PID 200 Hz 取自 P2 自查表；驱动器内环非你调。

### Q22. Explain sliding mode control and chattering. Why did you use it on the launcher?

**中文题意**：解释滑模控制和抖振。你为什么在发射机构上用它？
**考察点**：P3 的非线性控制。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：Sliding mode control defines a sliding surface, for example the error rate plus a constant times the error, and uses a switching term to drive the state onto that surface, where the error then decays. It is robust to bounded disturbances and model errors. The drawback is chattering, because the control switches rapidly, so a boundary layer or a smooth function replaces the sign function. On the launcher, each pellet loads the friction wheel and the speed drops. A plain PID recovered in about 120 milliseconds, and sliding mode with a boundary layer shortened this to about 60 to 80 milliseconds, with less spread in the muzzle velocity.
**中文翻译**：滑模控制定义一个滑模面，例如误差变化率加常数乘误差，并用切换项把状态驱动到该面上，误差随后在面上衰减。它对有界扰动和模型误差鲁棒。缺点是抖振，因为控制快速切换，所以用边界层或平滑函数代替符号函数。在发射机构上，每发弹丸都会给摩擦轮加载，转速下降。普通 PID 约 120 毫秒恢复，带边界层的滑模把这缩短到约 60 到 80 毫秒，弹速散布也更小。
**追问 1**：What is the boundary layer? — A thin band around the surface where the control is smooth instead of switching. *（中文：边界层是什么？— 滑模面附近的窄带，控制在其中平滑而不切换。）*
**追问 2**：What does a bigger boundary layer do? — Less chattering, but less robustness. *（中文：边界层更大会怎样？— 抖振更少，但鲁棒性降低。）*
**关键词与易错点**：sliding surface、boundary layer、chattering；60–80 ms 与 120 ms 取自 P3 自查表，说 about。

### Q23. How do you estimate the centre of mass of a robot with moving arms?

**中文题意**：带运动手臂的机器人质心如何估计？
**考察点**：P1 质心前馈的原理。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：The robot's centre of mass is the mass-weighted average of the centres of mass of all links. With a model from the URDF, giving each link's mass, inertial frame and position, I compute the forward kinematics from the arm joint angles and then take the weighted average. A library like Pinocchio does this directly. For the ballbot, the arm joints change the overall centre of mass, which shifts the balance point in roll and pitch. I fed this shift into the controller as gravity feedforward. The accuracy depends on the model, so I checked the total mass against a weighing and compared the response with the compensation on and off.
**中文翻译**：机器人的质心是各连杆质心的质量加权平均。有了来自 URDF 的模型，给出每个连杆的质量、惯性坐标系和位置，我由手臂关节角计算正运动学，再取加权平均。Pinocchio 这样的库可以直接做。对球形机器人，手臂关节会改变整体质心，从而在 roll 和 pitch 方向移动平衡点。我把这个偏移作为重力前馈输入控制器。精度取决于模型，所以我用称重核对总质量，并对比开关补偿时的响应。
**追问 1**：How close was the mass to the real one? — Within about five percent after checking. *（中文：质量与真实值多接近？— 核对后约在百分之五以内。）*
**追问 2**：How much did the centre of mass move? — About three to four centimetres with the arms raised in front. *（中文：质心移动多少？— 手臂前举时约三到四厘米。）*
**关键词与易错点**：mass-weighted average、URDF、Pinocchio；数字取自 P1 自查表（约 5%，3~4 cm），说 about。

### Q24. What is the difference between open-loop and closed-loop control? Give a robotics example from your work.

**中文题意**：开环与闭环控制有什么区别？举一个你工作中的机器人例子。
**考察点**：基础概念与应用。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：In open-loop control, the command is computed without measuring the result. In closed-loop control, we measure the output, compare it with the target, and correct. Open loop is simple but cannot reject disturbances; closed loop can, at the cost of possible instability and the need for sensors. An example from my work: a feedforward term such as gravity compensation is open loop, since it uses a model, while the wheel-speed PID on the chassis is closed loop with encoder feedback. Good designs combine both: feedforward to do the bulk of the work, and feedback to correct the remaining error.
**中文翻译**：开环控制中，命令的计算不测量结果。闭环控制中，我们测量输出、与目标比较并修正。开环简单，但不能抑制扰动；闭环可以，代价是可能失稳并需要传感器。我工作中的例子：重力补偿这样的前馈项是开环的，因为它用模型；底盘上的轮速 PID 是带编码器反馈的闭环。好的设计两者结合：前馈承担主要工作，反馈修正剩余误差。
**追问 1**：Why add feedforward to feedback? — It reduces the work feedback must do and speeds up the response. *（中文：为什么在反馈上加前馈？— 它减少反馈的工作并加快响应。）*
**追问 2**：Risk of feedforward? — A wrong model gives wrong compensation. *（中文：前馈的风险？— 模型错则补偿错。）*
**关键词与易错点**：feedforward + feedback；例子要具体；不要把重力补偿说成闭环。

### Q25. What questions would you like to ask us?

**中文题意**：你有什么问题想问我们？
**考察点**：反问质量。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I have three questions. First, how do students choose between the dissertation route and the coursework route, and how are supervisors matched? Second, are there laboratory or project facilities for robotics that students can use, for example for course projects? Third, given that I come from a computer science background, which courses or preparation would you recommend before the first semester? I would also be glad to hear how students link their studies with industry projects. I have read the programme page, but I would like to hear your view of how it works in practice.
**中文翻译**：我有三个问题。第一，学生如何在论文路线和课程路线之间选择，导师如何匹配？第二，是否有机器人实验室或项目设施供学生使用，例如用于课程项目？第三，鉴于我来自计算机背景，您建议我在第一个学期前做什么课程或准备？我也想知道学生如何把学习与产业项目联系起来。我已读过项目页面，但想听听您对实际运作的看法。
**追问 1**：Why ask about preparation? — Because I want to start the first semester ready. *（中文：为什么问准备？— 因为我想带着准备开始第一学期。）*
**追问 2**：Any question about fees? — I will check the official page. *（中文：关于学费的问题？— 我会查官网。）*
**关键词与易错点**：route、facilities、preparation；不问官网能查到的。

---

## 4. 第二部分：港城大 MSc in Computer Science（P53）（Q26–Q50）

### 4.1 项目一页要点与面试形式

| 项 | 内容（来源与等级） |
|---|---|
| 项目 | MSc in Computer Science（P53），香港城市大学；全日制 1 年；30 学分；主要为平日晚间课程（官方核对表） |
| 课程（据 PS 引用） | 核心：Software Engineering、Data Engineering、Computer Networks and Internets；选修流：Artificial Intelligence Stream（含 Machine Learning: Principles and Practice）、数据科学、信息安全；Project 课程或 Research/Internship Project（**2027/28 名称以官网核对**） |
| 面试 | 官方 FAQ：面试按个案安排；英语成绩刚达下限的申请人可能被安排英语面试；你的 IELTS 6.5 不属于典型情形，仍应准备简短英语交流。官方强调申请人需有大学水平的编程知识 |
| 与你的匹配 | CS 学位；平台软件（P6）、分布式通信、嵌入式 C/C++；AI 工程（P4）；数据结构与算法、操作系统课程 |
| 风险 | 你的叙事偏机器人，要说清“为什么用 CS 项目深化软件与系统”；技术题会落在编程基础 |

**面试前核对清单**：① 是否安排面试及形式（真人 / 录制）；② PS 点名课程在 2027/28 项目页的名称是否一致；③ 晚间授课的具体安排；④ 你提交的 PS 版本（长版还是短版）以实际提交为准。

**A. 项目匹配度与动机（Q26–Q30）**

### Q26. You have mostly done robotics. Why a Computer Science master's at CityU?

**中文题意**：你做的主要是机器人，为什么申请港城大计算机硕士？
**考察点**：动机一致性。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：Because the hardest problems in my robotics projects were software and systems problems: timestamps, state consistency, interfaces, communication and testing. At Kaihong, my work is largely platform software. From the programme page, my understanding is that the programme covers software engineering, data engineering, and computer networks, with an AI stream, which matches the skills I want to deepen. I will still use robotics as my application domain, but I want a stronger base in software design and systems. It is a one-year programme, which fits my wish to move into industry soon after graduation.
**中文翻译**：因为我机器人项目中最难的问题是软件和系统问题：时间戳、状态一致性、接口、通信和测试。在深开鸿，我的工作主要是平台软件。就我从项目页面了解的，项目涵盖软件工程、数据工程和计算机网络，并有 AI 方向，与我想深化的技能匹配。我仍会把机器人作为应用领域，但想在软件设计与系统方面有更强的基础。这是一年制项目，符合我毕业后尽快进入产业的愿望。
**追问 1**：Give a software problem from your robot work. — Wrong timestamps between sensors made the pose jump. *（中文：举一个机器人工作里的软件问题。— 传感器间时间戳错误让位姿跳变。）*
**追问 2**：Do you still want to work in robotics? — Yes, as an algorithm engineer. *（中文：仍想做机器人吗？— 是的，做算法工程师。）*
**关键词与易错点**：software and systems problems、one-year；课程名加“我了解到”。

### Q27. Why a one-year programme with evening classes?

**中文题意**：为什么选一年制、晚间授课的项目？
**考察点**：对项目形式的认识。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：A one-year programme matches my plan to enter industry soon after graduation, and I think I can learn efficiently with a focused schedule. From the information I have, courses are mainly in the evening on weekdays, and I will check the timetable. I can use the daytime for self-study, projects and, if allowed, relevant practice. I am used to a heavy schedule from combining study, competition and internships. I know that one year is intense, so I plan to prepare before arrival, especially in the networking and database topics where I have less background.
**中文翻译**：一年制符合我毕业后尽快进入产业的计划，我认为专注的节奏下我能高效学习。就我所了解，课程主要在工作日晚上，我会核对时间表。白天我可以自学、做项目，如果允许也做相关实践。我习惯学习、竞赛和实习并行的繁重日程。我知道一年很紧，所以计划入学前就准备，尤其是我背景较少的网络和数据库主题。
**追问 1**：What topics are you less prepared for? — Databases and advanced networking. *（中文：哪些主题准备较少？— 数据库和高级网络。）*
**追问 2**：Will you work during the programme? — Only if allowed and compatible. *（中文：学习期间会工作吗？— 仅在允许且不冲突时。）*
**关键词与易错点**：focused、prepare before arrival；不要承诺兼职。

### Q28. Why CityU in Hong Kong?

**中文题意**：为什么选香港城市大学？
**考察点**：学校与地区的理由。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I want a computer science programme with solid software engineering, data and networks, and an AI stream, in the Greater Bay Area, close to where I live and work. As far as I know, CityU's computer science department emphasises applied computing and has strong industry links, though I do not claim detailed knowledge. Hong Kong also provides an international English-speaking environment. The programme page says it expects university-level programming knowledge, which I have, and which I use daily in C, C++ and Python. I would like to learn more about project options once I am on campus.
**中文翻译**：我想在大湾区读一个软件工程、数据和网络扎实、并有 AI 方向的计算机项目，并靠近我生活和工作的地方。据我所知，港城大计算机系强调应用计算并有较强的产业联系，但我不声称有详细了解。香港也提供国际化的英语环境。项目页面说期望申请人有大学水平的编程知识，我具备，并每天用 C、C++ 和 Python。到校后我想进一步了解项目选项。
**追问 1**：What programming do you do daily? — C and C++ for embedded and robot code, Python for tools. *（中文：每天做什么编程？— 嵌入式和机器人代码用 C/C++，工具用 Python。）*
**追问 2**：Do you know the faculty? — Not by name yet. *（中文：认识教师吗？— 还不认识名字。）*
**关键词与易错点**：applied computing（as far as I know）、programming knowledge；不编教授名。

### Q29. Which stream would you choose: AI, data science or information security?

**中文题意**：你选哪个方向：AI、数据科学还是信息安全？
**考察点**：选修规划。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I would choose the artificial intelligence stream, since it matches my application field and my gap in machine learning theory; I understand that it includes a machine learning course on principles and practice, and I will verify the name. I would also like to take something on data, because the systems I build produce and depend on a lot of data. Information security interests me for the safe execution of commands in robot systems, so I would consider an introductory course if the schedule allows. I will decide after discussing the options with the course coordinator.
**中文翻译**：我会选人工智能方向，因为它符合我的应用领域和我在机器学习理论上的缺口；我了解到其中有一门关于原理与实践的机器学习课程，名称我会核对。我也想选与数据相关的课，因为我做的系统产生并依赖大量数据。信息安全对我有吸引力，因为机器人系统中命令要安全执行，所以如果时间允许，我会考虑入门课程。我会在与课程负责人讨论选项后决定。
**追问 1**：Why security? — Commands that move robots must be authenticated and checked. *（中文：为什么安全？— 让机器人动起来的命令必须认证和检查。）*
**追问 2**：Do you have security experience? — Only design-level, such as signed approval tickets and policy checks. *（中文：有安全经验吗？— 只有设计层面，如签名的批准凭据和策略检查。）*
**关键词与易错点**：AI stream、verify name；P6 票据为设计级描述；不夸大安全经验。

### Q30. Would you choose the project course or the research/internship project? Why?

**中文题意**：你选项目课还是研究/实习项目？为什么？
**考察点**：毕业项目偏好（据 PS 引用）。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I would prefer a project that has a clear deliverable and a real-world flavour, such as a software system for robots or a data pipeline, so I would consider the project course or an internship-type option, depending on what is available. A research project could also suit me, if I can find a well-defined question with a supervisor. In either case, I would define the requirements and the tests first, use version control, and document the work. I will check the exact options and requirements of the programme, because I do not want to assume they are the same as I understand now.
**中文翻译**：我更喜欢有明确交付物、有现实色彩的项目，比如机器人软件系统或数据流水线，所以会根据可选项考虑项目课或实习类选项。如果能与导师找到明确的问题，研究项目也适合我。无论哪种，我都会先定义需求和测试，使用版本控制，并写文档。我会核对项目的确切选项和要求，因为不想假设它们与我现在理解的一致。
**追问 1**：Example of a deliverable? — A tested software module with documentation and a demo. *（中文：交付物例子？— 经测试、有文档和演示的软件模块。）*
**追问 2**：How do you define success? — Requirements met, tests passing, and a clear report. *（中文：怎么定义成功？— 满足需求、测试通过、报告清楚。）*
**关键词与易错点**：deliverable、tests、document；选项名称以官网为准。

**B. 课程与研究方向（Q31–Q34）**

### Q31. What do you expect to learn in Software Engineering?

**中文题意**：你期望在“软件工程”里学到什么？
**考察点**：对课程的合理期待，不编造。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：From the programme page, my understanding is that there is a core course in software engineering. I have built software in projects, but mostly by experience. I expect to learn the formal side: requirements, design, architecture, testing, maintenance and project management, and how teams manage change. I already use version control, code review, unit tests with fake devices, and contract tests for interfaces on my platform work. I want to understand why these practices work and how to apply them at scale. I would also like to learn about design patterns and how to measure software quality.
**中文翻译**：就我从项目页面了解的，核心里有软件工程课程。我在项目里做过软件，但主要靠经验。我期望学到形式化的一面：需求、设计、架构、测试、维护和项目管理，以及团队如何管理变更。我在平台工作中已经使用版本控制、代码评审、带假设备的单元测试和接口契约测试。我想理解这些做法为何有效，以及如何大规模应用。我也想学习设计模式和如何度量软件质量。
**追问 1**：What is a contract test? — A test that checks that an implementation satisfies an interface specification. *（中文：什么是契约测试？— 检查实现是否满足接口规范的测试。）*
**追问 2**：Which pattern did you use? — Adapter and publish–subscribe. *（中文：用了哪种模式？— 适配器和发布订阅。）*
**关键词与易错点**：requirements–design–test；课程名“我了解到”；P6 测试为设计级描述。

### Q32. What interests you in Data Engineering?

**中文题意**：你对“数据工程”有什么兴趣？
**考察点**：数据管道意识。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：My robot systems produce streams of data: sensor states, logs, images and command records. I have handled data flow, standardised timestamps and frames, validity checks, recording and replay of data for debugging. In the gardening project, I merged vision observations, sensor data and user commands into one traceable system state. I want to learn data engineering more formally: storage, pipelines, quality checks, schemas and scalable processing. This would help me build the data side of robot systems, such as logging, evaluation datasets and monitoring. I have limited experience with databases, and I want to build that skill.
**中文翻译**：我的机器人系统产生数据流：传感器状态、日志、图像和命令记录。我处理过数据流，标准化时间戳和坐标系，做有效性检查，并录制与回放数据用于调试。在园艺项目里，我把视觉观测、传感数据和用户命令合并成一个可追溯的系统状态。我想更正式地学习数据工程：存储、流水线、质量检查、模式和可扩展处理。这有助于我构建机器人系统的数据侧，如日志、评估数据集和监控。我在数据库方面经验有限，想培养这项技能。
**追问 1**：What is a schema? — A defined structure of the data fields and types. *（中文：什么是模式（schema）？— 数据字段与类型的既定结构。）*
**追问 2**：What data quality check did you apply? — Range, finite values, timestamp monotonicity and freshness. *（中文：做过什么数据质量检查？— 范围、有限值、时间戳单调性和新鲜度。）*
**关键词与易错点**：pipeline、validity checks；诚实说数据库经验有限；不要夸大规模。

### Q33. What interests you in Computer Networks and Internets?

**中文题意**：你对“计算机网络与互联网”有什么兴趣？
**考察点**：网络基础与经历。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：In my work, communication is everywhere: CAN between motor controllers, sockets between devices and servers in my gardening system, and publish–subscribe middleware between robots at Kaihong. I have seen how delay, packet loss and bandwidth limits affect behaviour: for example, bus load on CAN, and the need to use best-effort delivery for fast state data and reliable delivery for commands. I want to learn the Internet layers and protocols more systematically: TCP and UDP, routing, congestion control, and application protocols. This would help me design more robust distributed robot software.
**中文翻译**：在我的工作中通信无处不在：电机控制器之间的 CAN、园艺系统里设备和服务器之间的套接字，以及深开鸿机器人之间的发布订阅中间件。我见过延迟、丢包和带宽限制如何影响行为：例如 CAN 的总线负载，以及快速状态数据用尽力而为、命令用可靠传输的需要。我想更系统地学习互联网分层和协议：TCP 和 UDP、路由、拥塞控制和应用层协议。这有助于我设计更健壮的分布式机器人软件。
**追问 1**：What is congestion control? — Mechanisms that slow the sender when the network is overloaded. *（中文：什么是拥塞控制？— 网络过载时减缓发送方的机制。）*
**追问 2**：Why best-effort for state? — The latest sample matters most, so retransmitting old ones is wasteful. *（中文：为什么状态用尽力而为？— 最新样本最重要，重传旧样本浪费。）*
**关键词与易错点**：TCP/UDP、bus load；QoS 设置为设计级描述；CAN 在 P7 是经典 CAN。

### Q34. What do you want to learn in Machine Learning: Principles and Practice?

**中文题意**：你想在“机器学习：原理与实践”里学什么？
**考察点**：ML 理论缺口，并核对课程名。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I understand that there is a machine learning course in the AI stream, and I will verify the exact title. I have trained and deployed a detector, so I know the practice side: data, training, evaluation and deployment. What I want is the principles: the maths of learning, generalisation, regularisation, optimisation and probabilistic views, and a systematic way to choose and compare models. I also want to learn about the limits of models, such as bias and robustness. This would help me judge machine learning methods critically when I use them in robot systems.
**中文翻译**：我了解到 AI 方向有一门机器学习课程，具体标题我会核对。我训练并部署过检测器，所以懂实践侧：数据、训练、评估和部署。我想要的是原理：学习的数学、泛化、正则化、优化和概率视角，以及选择和比较模型的系统方法。我也想学习模型的局限，如偏见和鲁棒性。这将帮助我在机器人系统中使用机器学习方法时进行批判性评价。
**追问 1**：What is regularisation? — Adding a penalty or constraint to reduce overfitting. *（中文：什么是正则化？— 加惩罚或约束以减少过拟合。）*
**追问 2**：How do you choose between models? — Validation performance, complexity and deployment constraints. *（中文：怎么选模型？— 验证表现、复杂度和部署限制。）*
**关键词与易错点**：principles、generalisation；课程名“核对后再说”；你的 ML 以实践为主。

**C. 该校面试形式可能出现的题（Q35–Q37）**

### Q35. Briefly describe one project you are proud of, in under two minutes, as in a short interview.

**中文题意**：像短面谈一样，两分钟内简述一个你自豪的项目。
**考察点**：15–20 分钟短面谈的项目叙述。
**English answer（约 120 词）**：I am proud of the platform work at Kaihong, because it is about making robot systems safe and reusable. I built a unified capability model for sensors, actuators and controllers, so motion code can be reused across hardware, and I mapped ROS and ROS2 commands to low-level drivers. I standardised timestamps, frames and validity checks so the control layer gets a trusted state. I also implemented device discovery and task dispatch over a publish–subscribe middleware, and a chain that checks task plans before execution: proposal, policy check, execution, feedback. It is ongoing, and I can talk about the design and my modules, not internal code. It taught me how important interfaces and testing are.
**中文翻译**：我为深开鸿的平台工作感到自豪，因为它关乎让机器人系统安全且可复用。我做了传感器、执行器和控制器的统一能力模型，使运动代码可跨硬件复用，并把 ROS 和 ROS2 命令映射到底层驱动。我标准化了时间戳、坐标系和有效性检查，让控制层得到可信的状态。我还在发布订阅中间件上实现了设备发现和任务分发，以及在执行前检查任务计划的链条：提案、策略检查、执行、反馈。工作仍在进行，我可以讲设计和我的模块，不讲内部代码。它让我明白接口和测试多么重要。
**追问 1**：What is the hardest part? — Keeping states consistent across devices and time. *（中文：最难的是什么？— 在设备和时间之间保持状态一致。）*
**追问 2**：What do you test? — Adapters with the same contract tests, and faults such as delay and packet loss. *（中文：测试什么？— 用同一套契约测试测适配器，以及延迟、丢包等故障。）*
**关键词与易错点**：capability model、trusted state；保密边界；P6 仍在进行，用 Present。

### Q36. A short English conversation: what would you do on your first day as a software engineer on a team?

**中文题意**：英语短交流：作为软件工程师加入团队的第一天，你会做什么？
**考察点**：英语表达与工程素养（英语面试可能用轻松问题）。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I would first read the documentation and the main parts of the code, set up the development environment, and run the tests to see the starting point. I would ask my teammates about the goals of the project, the main interfaces, and how the team works: code review, branches and releases. Then I would pick a small task, such as fixing a minor bug or adding a test, so that I learn the workflow quickly and make a contribution early. I would keep notes of questions, and I would not be afraid to ask them.
**中文翻译**：我会先读文档和代码的主要部分，搭建开发环境，运行测试看起点。我会向队友了解项目目标、主要接口，以及团队的工作方式：代码评审、分支和发布。然后选一个小任务，比如修一个小 bug 或补一个测试，快速学习工作流程并尽早做出贡献。我会记下问题，并且不怕提问。
**追问 1**：How do you handle an unclear task? — I restate it in writing and confirm with the owner. *（中文：任务不清楚怎么办？— 书面复述并与负责人确认。）*
**追问 2**：Your preferred way to learn a new codebase? — Run it, read the tests, trace one feature end to end. *（中文：学新代码库的方式？— 运行它、读测试、端到端追踪一个功能。）*
**关键词与易错点**：environment、tests、small task；回答简洁自然；不要背稿。

### Q37. What do you think about AI tools that write code? Will they change the job of a software engineer?

**中文题意**：你怎么看会写代码的 AI 工具？会改变软件工程师的工作吗？
**考察点**：社会热点类问题（二手信息称港城大可能问）。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I think they will change the way we work, but not remove the need for engineers. They are good at generating routine code and suggestions. But someone must define the requirements, review the code, test it and take responsibility, especially for systems that control hardware. Generated code can contain subtle errors, so tests and review become more important. The skills that gain value are design, verification and understanding of the system. For me, I use such tools carefully as an assistant, and I check everything against tests. I do not have exact figures on the effect on jobs, so this is my view from experience.
**中文翻译**：我认为它们会改变工作方式，但不会消除对工程师的需求。它们擅长生成常规代码和建议。但仍需有人定义需求、评审代码、测试并承担责任，特别是控制硬件的系统。生成的代码可能含有细微错误，所以测试和评审变得更重要。升值的技能是设计、验证和对系统的理解。我自己会谨慎地把这类工具当作助手，并用测试检查一切。我没有关于就业影响的确切数字，这是基于经验的看法。
**追问 1**：Do you use them yourself? — As an assistant, with tests and review. *（中文：你自己用吗？— 作为助手使用，并有测试和评审。）*
**追问 2**：What about academic integrity? — I follow the course rules and say clearly what I used. *（中文：学术诚信呢？— 遵守课程规则，并说明用了什么。）*
**关键词与易错点**：change not remove、verification；不要说“AI will replace programmers”；不引用无法核实的数据。

**D. 技术考点预测（Q38–Q47）**

### Q38. Write down binary search in words, and give its complexity and common bugs.

**中文题意**：口述二分查找，并说复杂度和常见错误。
**考察点**：编程基础。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：Binary search works on a sorted array. Keep two indices, low and high. While low is not greater than high, compute the middle index, compare the middle element with the target. If equal, return it. If the middle is smaller, search the right half by moving low to middle plus one; otherwise search the left half by moving high to middle minus one. The time is order log n and the space is constant. Common bugs: an off-by-one error in the bounds, an infinite loop when the bounds do not shrink, and integer overflow when computing the middle as low plus high; use low plus half the difference instead.
**中文翻译**：二分查找作用于已排序数组。保留两个下标 low 和 high。当 low 不大于 high 时，计算中间下标，把中间元素与目标比较。相等就返回。若中间值较小，把 low 移到 mid+1 搜索右半；否则把 high 移到 mid−1 搜索左半。时间 O(log n)，空间 O(1)。常见错误：边界差一错误、边界不收缩造成的死循环，以及用 low+high 计算中间值时的整数溢出；应改用 low 加差的一半。
**追问 1**：Where would you use it in robotics? — Finding the interval in a sorted lookup table, such as scheduled gains by leg length. *（中文：机器人里哪用？— 在排序查表里找区间，如按腿长调度的增益。）*
**追问 2**：What if the array has duplicates? — Use a lower-bound variant to find the first occurrence. *（中文：有重复元素呢？— 用 lower-bound 变体找第一个出现位置。）*
**关键词与易错点**：sorted、mid、off-by-one、O(log n)；口述时先说不变量；不要忘了溢出。

### Q39. When would you choose a stack, a queue or a priority queue? Give examples from your work.

**中文题意**：什么时候用栈、队列或优先队列？结合你的工作举例。
**考察点**：数据结构选择。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：A stack is last in, first out: use it for undo, depth-first search, and nested calls. A queue is first in, first out: use it for passing messages and for breadth-first search. A priority queue returns the item with the highest priority, usually implemented as a heap with log n insertion and removal: use it for Dijkstra and A*, where we always expand the node with the lowest cost, and for task scheduling. In my work, a message queue passes sensor data between tasks, A*-style planners use a priority queue, and in my gardening server, an event queue with a maximum size drops the oldest events when it is full.
**中文翻译**：栈是后进先出：用于撤销、深度优先搜索和嵌套调用。队列是先进先出：用于传递消息和广度优先搜索。优先队列返回优先级最高的元素，通常用堆实现，插入和删除为 O(log n)：用于 Dijkstra 和 A*，每次扩展代价最低的节点，也用于任务调度。在我的工作中，消息队列在任务间传递传感器数据，A* 类规划器使用优先队列，在我的园艺服务端，有最大长度的事件队列在满时丢弃最旧事件。
**追问 1**：What is a heap? — A complete binary tree where each parent is smaller (or larger) than its children. *（中文：什么是堆？— 每个父节点小于（或大于）其子节点的完全二叉树。）*
**追问 2**：Why drop the oldest event? — The newest state matters more for monitoring. *（中文：为什么丢最旧事件？— 监控中最新状态更重要。）*
**关键词与易错点**：LIFO/FIFO、heap、O(log n)；队列长度 100 取自 P4 自查表；不要说 heap 就是排序数组。

### Q40. Explain balanced binary search trees and database indexes.

**中文题意**：解释平衡二叉搜索树和数据库索引。
**考察点**：数据结构与数据工程。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：A binary search tree keeps smaller keys on the left and larger on the right, so search, insert and delete are order of the height. If the tree is unbalanced, the height can be n, so balanced trees, such as AVL or red-black trees, keep the height at log n using rotations. Databases typically use B-trees or B plus trees for indexes. They have many keys per node, so the tree is shallow, and each node fits a disk page, which reduces disk reads. An index speeds up lookups and range queries, at the cost of more storage and slower writes. I know this at the concept level from my data structures course.
**中文翻译**：二叉搜索树把较小的键放左边、较大的放右边，所以查找、插入、删除为树高量级。若树不平衡，高度可达 n，所以平衡树，如 AVL 或红黑树，用旋转保持高度为 log n。数据库的索引通常用 B 树或 B+ 树。每个节点有许多键，所以树很浅，每个节点对应一个磁盘页，减少磁盘读取。索引加快查找和范围查询，代价是更多存储和更慢的写入。我是从数据结构课程中在概念层面了解的。
**追问 1**：Why B-trees for disks? — They minimise the number of page reads. *（中文：为什么磁盘用 B 树？— 最小化页读取次数。）*
**追问 2**：Do you have database experience? — Limited; I have used simple storage, and I want to learn properly. *（中文：有数据库经验吗？— 有限，用过简单存储，想认真学。）*
**关键词与易错点**：height、rotation、B+ tree；概念水平；诚实说数据库经验有限。

### Q41. Explain object-oriented programming and an example from your adapters or modules.

**中文题意**：解释面向对象编程，并结合你的适配器或模块举例。
**考察点**：软件设计。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：Object-oriented programming organises code into objects that bundle data and behaviour. Its main ideas are encapsulation, hiding internals behind an interface; inheritance, reusing and extending behaviour; and polymorphism, using different implementations through the same interface. In the device layer of my platform work, each device type has an adapter that implements the same capability interface: send a velocity command, read a state. The upper layers use the interface and do not care whether the device is a wheeled base or an arm, which lets the same motion code run on different hardware. I prefer composition and clear interfaces over deep inheritance.
**中文翻译**：面向对象编程把代码组织成把数据和行为打包的对象。主要思想是封装，把内部细节隐藏在接口后；继承，复用和扩展行为；多态，通过同一接口使用不同实现。在我平台工作的设备层，每种设备类型都有一个实现同一能力接口的适配器：发送速度命令、读取状态。上层使用接口，不关心设备是轮式底盘还是机械臂，使同一运动代码能在不同硬件上运行。我更偏好组合和清晰接口，而不是深层继承。
**追问 1**：Why composition over inheritance? — It reduces coupling and makes changes easier. *（中文：为什么偏好组合？— 降低耦合，更易修改。）*
**追问 2**：What is a virtual function? — A function whose implementation is chosen at run time by the object's type. *（中文：什么是虚函数？— 实现由对象类型在运行时选择的函数。）*
**关键词与易错点**：encapsulation、polymorphism、adapter；设计级描述；不透露内部代码。

### Q42. What design patterns have you used or know?

**中文题意**：你用过或了解哪些设计模式？
**考察点**：软件工程知识。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I have used three in practice. The adapter pattern: a wrapper that makes different devices look like one capability interface. The observer or publish–subscribe pattern: components publish state and others subscribe, which decouples producers and consumers. The state machine, which is more a design idea than a pattern: the pump controller in my gardening system has states and transitions with separate start and stop thresholds. I also know others at the concept level, such as the factory, the singleton and the strategy pattern, and I would like to study when to use them and when not to.
**中文翻译**：我在实践中用过三个。适配器模式：封装器，使不同设备看起来像同一个能力接口。观察者或发布订阅模式：组件发布状态，其他组件订阅，使生产者和消费者解耦。状态机，更像设计思想而不是模式：园艺系统的水泵控制器有状态和转移，起停阈值分开。我也在概念层面了解其他模式，如工厂、单例和策略，想学习何时该用、何时不该用。
**追问 1**：What is the risk of singletons? — Hidden global state makes testing harder. *（中文：单例的风险？— 隐藏的全局状态使测试更难。）*
**追问 2**：Strategy pattern example? — Switching between different controllers behind one interface. *（中文：策略模式例子？— 在同一接口后切换不同控制器。）*
**关键词与易错点**：adapter、pub-sub、state machine；不要列太多没用过的模式。

### Q43. How do you design a REST API versus a WebSocket interface for a monitoring page?

**中文题意**：为监控页面设计 REST API 与 WebSocket 接口有什么区别？
**考察点**：Web/网络应用基础（官方：web 应用知识优先）。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：REST uses HTTP requests with resources and standard methods: get a state, post a command. It is simple, cacheable and stateless, good for occasional queries and commands. WebSocket keeps a persistent two-way connection, so the server can push updates immediately, which suits live monitoring, like sensor values and robot state. In my gardening project, the web page connects over a WebSocket to show live state and send user commands, with a separate static page served over HTTP. I would use REST for configuration and history, and WebSocket for live updates. Both need input validation and authentication in a real system.
**中文翻译**：REST 用带资源和标准方法的 HTTP 请求：获取状态、提交命令。它简单、可缓存、无状态，适合偶尔的查询和命令。WebSocket 保持持久的双向连接，服务器可以立即推送更新，适合实时监控，如传感器值和机器人状态。在我的园艺项目里，网页通过 WebSocket 连接来显示实时状态并发送用户命令，另有通过 HTTP 提供的静态页面。我会用 REST 做配置和历史，用 WebSocket 做实时更新。真实系统里两者都需要输入校验和认证。
**追问 1**：What does stateless mean? — Each request carries all information needed; the server keeps no session state. *（中文：无状态是什么意思？— 每个请求带齐所需信息，服务器不保留会话状态。）*
**追问 2**：What if the connection drops? — Reconnect with backoff and resynchronise the state. *（中文：连接断了呢？— 带退避重连并重新同步状态。）*
**关键词与易错点**：request/response vs push、validation；P4 用 websockets 库、端口 8765；不要夸大前端经验。

### Q44. How would you design an idempotent command interface, and why?

**中文题意**：如何设计幂等的命令接口，为什么？
**考察点**：分布式系统设计（P6）。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：Networks can fail and retry, so a command may arrive more than once. If the command is "move forward one metre", executing it twice is a problem. An idempotent design gives each command a unique key. The receiver stores the keys it has seen for a time window, and if a key arrives again, it returns the previous result and does not execute again. The window should be longer than the retry and validity time. Commands should also carry a validity period, so old ones are rejected. In my platform work, I used the same idea: at-least-once delivery with deduplication by key, which fixed a bug where tasks were sent twice after a reconnection.
**中文翻译**：网络可能失败并重试，所以命令可能到达不止一次。如果命令是“前进一米”，执行两次就有问题。幂等设计给每条命令一个唯一的键。接收方在一个时间窗口内存储见过的键，若同一个键再来，就返回之前的结果而不再执行。窗口应长于重试和有效期。命令还应带有效期，使旧命令被拒绝。在我的平台工作里，我用了同样思想：至少一次投递加按键去重，修复了重连后任务被发送两次的 bug。
**追问 1**：How long do you keep the keys? — About twice the validity period of a command. *（中文：键保留多久？— 约为命令有效期的两倍。）*
**追问 2**：What if the receiver restarts? — Keep the table on disk, or stay quiet for one window before accepting new commands. *（中文：接收方重启呢？— 把表存盘，或在一个窗口内先不接新命令。）*
**关键词与易错点**：idempotency key、window、validity；设计取值取自 P6 自查表，说 “in our design”。

### Q45. How do you structure logging and monitoring in a system with several services?

**中文题意**：多服务系统里你如何设计日志和监控？
**考察点**：工程实践。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I use structured logs with a timestamp, a source, a level, an identifier that follows a request across services, and a reason code for decisions. I keep high-rate data in recordings that can be replayed, and low-rate summaries for dashboards, for example the age of the data, the loop period, and error counters. For faults, I use an event-triggered buffer that saves the last second or so before the event. Alerts go on a few clear conditions, such as missing heartbeats or a loop period over a threshold. I also record the version and configuration with each run, so a result can be reproduced.
**中文翻译**：我使用结构化日志，包含时间戳、来源、级别、跟随请求穿过各服务的标识，以及决策的原因码。高频数据放在可回放的录制中，低频摘要用于仪表盘，例如数据年龄、循环周期和错误计数。对故障，我用事件触发的缓冲，保存事件前约一秒的数据。告警设在少数明确条件上，如缺失心跳或循环周期超阈值。我还随每次运行记录版本和配置，使结果可复现。
**追问 1**：What is a correlation id? — An identifier carried by all messages of one request, to trace it across services. *（中文：什么是关联 id？— 一个请求的所有消息携带的标识，用于跨服务追踪。）*
**追问 2**：Why version and config in each run? — To reproduce and compare results. *（中文：为什么每次记录版本和配置？— 为了复现和比较结果。）*
**关键词与易错点**：structured logs、replay、alerts；日志做法取自 P1/P2/P6 的自查表；说 “in my projects”。

### Q46. How would you test a function that depends on time, such as a timeout or a watchdog?

**中文题意**：如何测试依赖时间的函数，如超时或看门狗？
**考察点**：测试设计。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I make the clock an input and not a global. In production, the function uses the real clock, and in tests I pass a fake clock that I can advance step by step. Then I can test: the command arrives, the clock advances less than the timeout, nothing happens; the clock advances past the timeout, the stop action is triggered. I also test the boundaries, such as exactly at the timeout, and odd cases such as the clock jumping backwards. This makes tests fast and repeatable, and not dependent on real waiting. I used this approach for the command watchdog in my platform work.
**中文翻译**：我把时钟作为输入而不是全局。生产中函数使用真实时钟，测试中传入可逐步推进的假时钟。这样我可以测试：命令到达，时钟推进不到超时，什么都不发生；时钟推进超过超时，触发停止动作。我也测试边界，如恰好在超时点，以及奇怪的情形，如时钟回拨。这使测试快速且可重复，不依赖真实等待。我在平台工作中对命令看门狗用了这种方法。
**追问 1**：Why test a clock jump? — A device clock can jump, and the logic must not misjudge freshness. *（中文：为什么测试时钟跳变？— 设备时钟可能跳变，逻辑不能误判新鲜度。）*
**追问 2**：What timeout did you use? — About four times the command period, with a two-strike rule. *（中文：超时设多少？— 约为命令周期的四倍，且连续两次判定。）*
**关键词与易错点**：fake clock、boundaries；数值取自 P6 自查表，说 about。

### Q47. How do you reason about the performance of a program? Give an example where you improved it.

**中文题意**：如何分析程序性能？举一个你优化过的例子。
**考察点**：性能意识。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：I measure first, then change. I look at timing per stage, and at the distribution and not only the average, because rare slow cases matter in real-time code. On the ballbot, the control loop period had occasional jitter. I logged a timestamp in each callback and computed the histogram of the differences offline. Using this, I reduced the jitter: the 99th percentile fell from about 3.5 to about 1 millisecond, and events over five milliseconds in an hour went from about 180 to none, by running the control node at a real-time priority pinned to one core, and keeping logging out of the control period. The lesson is to have a number before and after.
**中文翻译**：我先测量，再改动。我看每个阶段的耗时，并看分布而不只是平均值，因为在实时代码中罕见的慢情形很重要。在球形机器人上，控制循环周期偶尔有抖动。我在每个回调里记录时间戳，离线计算差值的直方图。据此我降低了抖动：99 分位从约 3.5 毫秒降到约 1 毫秒，一小时内超过 5 毫秒的事件从约 180 次降到零，做法是让控制节点以实时优先级运行并绑定到一个核，同时不在控制周期内打印日志。教训是要有优化前后的数字。
**追问 1**：Why the 99th percentile? — Worst cases break real-time guarantees; averages hide them. *（中文：为什么看 99 分位？— 最坏情形会破坏实时保证，平均值会掩盖。）*
**追问 2**：What else helped? — No logging inside the control period, and other nodes on different cores. *（中文：还有什么帮助？— 控制周期内不打日志，其他节点放在别的核上。）*
**关键词与易错点**：measure first、percentile；数字取自 P1 自查表（99 分位 3.5→1.0 ms；约 180 次→0），说 about。

**E. 项目深挖与收尾（Q48–Q50）**

### Q48. What did you learn from combining internships with your degree, and what would you change?

**中文题意**：你从实习与学业并行中学到什么？会改变什么？
**考察点**：反思与成长。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I learned that real projects teach different things from courses. In courses, the problem is well defined, but in internships, I had to define the problem, handle incomplete information and deliver something working. I learned to write down interfaces, to test early and to explain my decisions to colleagues. If I changed anything, I would start documenting my reasoning earlier, because I sometimes recorded it too late. And I would learn the theory in parallel with the practice, which is part of my reason for applying for this degree.
**中文翻译**：我学到真实项目教的东西与课程不同。课程里问题定义清楚，而实习中我必须定义问题、处理不完整的信息并交付可用的东西。我学会了写下接口、尽早测试，并向同事解释我的决定。如果要改变什么，我会更早地记录推理，因为我有时记得太晚。我也会让理论与实践并行学习，这是我申请这个学位的部分原因。
**追问 1**：How do you document reasoning? — Short notes with the decision, alternatives and the reason. *（中文：怎么记录推理？— 简短笔记，写明决定、备选和理由。）*
**追问 2**：Which internship taught you the most? — AIRS, for deployment on an unstable robot. *（中文：哪个实习学到最多？— AIRS，在不稳定机器人上部署。）*
**关键词与易错点**：define the problem、interfaces；时间线与 CV 一致；不夸大。

### Q49. How do you plan to use this degree in your career in the Greater Bay Area?

**中文题意**：你打算如何在大湾区的职业中使用这个学位？
**考察点**：职业规划。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I plan to work as a robotics software and algorithm engineer in the Greater Bay Area. This degree would give me stronger software engineering, data and networking foundations, plus machine learning principles, so that I can design the software side of robot products: interfaces, state management, communication, testing and the integration of AI components. In two to three years I hope to own a software module of a robot system, and later to lead the technical design of a platform. I would also use the one-year timeline to look for internships and projects in Hong Kong and Shenzhen.
**中文翻译**：我计划在大湾区做机器人软件和算法工程师。这个学位将给我更强的软件工程、数据和网络基础，以及机器学习原理，使我能设计机器人产品的软件侧：接口、状态管理、通信、测试和 AI 组件的集成。两三年内，我希望负责机器人系统的一个软件模块，之后带领平台的技术设计。我也会利用一年的时间线，在香港和深圳寻找实习和项目。
**追问 1**：Would you start your own company? — Not as a first goal. *（中文：会创业吗？— 不作为第一目标。）*
**追问 2**：Which skills first? — System design and testing. *（中文：先要哪些技能？— 系统设计和测试。）*
**关键词与易错点**：software side of robot products；不承诺具体公司；时间线合理。

### Q50. What questions would you like to ask us?

**中文题意**：你有什么问题想问我们？
**考察点**：反问质量。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I have three questions. First, how are the evening courses organised across the week, and how do students typically manage the project course alongside them? Second, what are the options for the final project, for example a research project or an internship-type project, and how are they assigned? Third, what background do successful applicants from outside software companies usually have, and what would you recommend I prepare before the first semester? I have read the official page and would like to hear your view of how it works in practice.
**中文翻译**：我有三个问题。第一，晚间课程在一周中如何安排，学生通常怎样兼顾项目课？第二，毕业项目有哪些选项，例如研究项目或实习类项目，如何分配？第三，来自软件公司之外的成功申请人通常有什么背景，您建议我在第一学期前准备什么？我已读过官网，想听您对实际运作的看法。
**追问 1**：Why ask about preparation? — To be ready on day one. *（中文：为什么问准备？— 为了第一天就准备好。）*
**追问 2**：Any question about fees? — I would check the official page. *（中文：关于学费？— 我会查官网。）*
**关键词与易错点**：practical questions；不问官网可查的信息。

---

## 5. 第三部分：港城大（东莞）MSc in Artificial Intelligence（Q51–Q75）

### 5.1 项目一页要点与面试形式

| 项 | 内容（来源与等级） |
|---|---|
| 项目 | MSc in Artificial Intelligence，City University of Hong Kong (Dongguan)；全日制 2 年（最长 3 年）（2026 轮次页面，仅作参考） |
| 官方培养目标（只引用这些） | 把 AI 的技术理解与实践理解结合；学习成果：应用 AI 开发工具并提出方案、设计高质量 AI 系统、识别特定 AI 领域的关键问题并加以解决 |
| 课程 | **本仓库材料中没有具体课程名单**：面试里不点具体课程名，只说培养目标；被问到时说“我看到的是培养目标，选课细节我会入学后与导师确认” |
| 面试形式 | 官网未列固定面试；官方 FAQ 允许跨学科申请并需要面试；第三方 2026 报道称为英文 AI 视频面试（录制式、限时作答），**均未经学校确认**，以邀请邮件为准。因此回答要自带结构：一句结论 → 一个具体例子 → 一个收获 |
| 与你的匹配 | P4（YOLOv5/RKNN + LangGraph + 灌溉闭环）是最直接的 AI 项目；P6 的 AI 决策到物理动作；PS 说希望做机器人学习项目（未承诺） |
| 风险 | 这是通用 AI 硕士，不是机器人专门方向；你的机器学习以实践为主，可能被追问理论 |

**面试前核对清单**：① 是否开放 2027/28、截止日期；② 面试是真人还是录制式，单题时长、准备时间、可否重录；③ 课程名单与毕业项目安排；④ 与同校 CS 的区分逻辑（见 Q76）。

**A. 项目匹配度与动机（Q51–Q56）**

### Q51. Why Artificial Intelligence at CityU (Dongguan)?

**中文题意**：为什么申请港城大东莞的人工智能硕士？
**考察点**：动机与项目培养目标的对应。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：The programme's aims, as I understand them, are to combine technical and practical understanding of AI, to apply AI development tools, to design high-quality AI systems, and to solve key problems in specific AI domains. This matches how I want to grow. I have built AI-related systems in practice, a vision detector and a decision chain, and I want a stronger conceptual base so that I can design them well and judge their limits. My application domain is robotics, and I want to use the degree to connect AI methods to physical systems. I will check the course list on the official page, and I do not want to guess at course titles.
**中文翻译**：据我理解，项目的目标是结合对 AI 的技术理解与实践理解、应用 AI 开发工具、设计高质量的 AI 系统，以及解决特定 AI 领域的关键问题。这与我想成长的方向一致。我在实践中做过与 AI 相关的系统，一个视觉检测器和一条决策链，想要更强的概念基础，以便把它们设计好并判断其局限。我的应用领域是机器人，我想用这个学位把 AI 方法与物理系统连起来。我会在官网核对课程列表，不想猜课程名。
**追问 1**：What AI systems have you built? — A plant-condition detector on an edge chip and a decision chain with a language model. *（中文：做过哪些 AI 系统？— 边缘芯片上的植物状态检测器和带语言模型的决策链。）*
**追问 2**：Which course attracts you? — I will confirm the list first; I am interested in learning-based methods for perception and decision. *（中文：哪门课吸引你？— 我先核对列表；我对感知和决策的学习方法感兴趣。）*
**关键词与易错点**：programme aims、practical + conceptual；不点课程名；不夸大 AI 经验。

### Q52. Why the Dongguan campus?

**中文题意**：为什么选东莞校区？
**考察点**：务实理由（调研建议准备）。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：There are practical reasons. Dongguan is in the Greater Bay Area, close to Shenzhen, where I live and work, so I can keep my connections and learn from the local technology and manufacturing industry. The programme is two years, which gives me time for both coursework and a project. I also see the benefit of a newer campus with a focused set of programmes, though I do not claim detailed knowledge yet. If I get an offer, I will visit the campus, talk to students, and ask about facilities, supervision and industry projects.
**中文翻译**：有务实的理由。东莞在大湾区，靠近我生活和工作的深圳，所以我可以保持联系，向本地的科技和制造业学习。项目为两年，给我时间同时完成课程和项目。我也看到较新校区、项目较聚焦的好处，但我暂时不声称有详细了解。如果拿到 offer，我会去参观校区、与学生交流，并询问设施、指导和产业项目。
**追问 1**：Would you live on campus? — I would consider it and check the options. *（中文：会住校吗？— 会考虑，并核对选项。）*
**追问 2**：Have you been to Dongguan? — Not for this purpose; I would visit. *（中文：去过东莞吗？— 没有为此去过，会去参观。）*
**关键词与易错点**：practical、Greater Bay Area；不贬低其他校区；没去过就说没去过。

### Q53. You are from computer science. Why do you want a master's in AI and not computer science at the same campus?

**中文题意**：你是计算机背景。为什么同校区选 AI 而不是计算机科学？
**考察点**：同校两个项目的区分。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I applied to both, and they answer different needs. The AI programme focuses on AI concepts, development tools and system design, which is what I need to turn my practical experience into solid foundations. The computer science programme focuses on software development, networks and data engineering, which suits my platform software side. My statement for the AI programme is about learning-based methods and how AI decisions become reliable actions. If I joined the AI programme, I would still use my software skills, and I would learn machine learning in depth. I will be clear in each interview about why I chose it.
**中文翻译**：我两个都申请了，它们回答不同的需求。AI 项目聚焦 AI 概念、开发工具和系统设计，这是我把实践经验变成扎实基础所需要的。计算机项目聚焦软件开发、网络和数据工程，适合我的平台软件一侧。我为 AI 项目写的陈述是关于基于学习的方法，以及 AI 决策如何变成可靠的动作。如果进入 AI 项目，我仍会用我的软件能力，并深入学习机器学习。每次面试我都会说清楚为什么选它。
**追问 1**：Which one is your priority? — I do not want to rank in an interview; this one fits my plan very well. *（中文：优先哪个？— 我不想在面试中排名；这个非常符合我的计划。）*
**追问 2**：Do both statements say the same? — The facts are the same, but the emphasis differs. *（中文：两份陈述一样吗？— 事实相同，侧重不同。）*
**关键词与易错点**：different needs；口径一致；不排名。

### Q54. This programme accepts students from different backgrounds. What is your relevant preparation?

**中文题意**：项目接收不同背景的学生。你有哪些相关准备？
**考察点**：跨学科申请的准备度（官方 FAQ 允许跨学科）。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：My degree is computer science, so I have programming, data structures, algorithms, linear algebra and probability from my courses. For AI, I trained a YOLOv5 detector, collected and labelled about 1,800 plant images, quantised it to INT8 for an edge chip, and built a decision chain with LangGraph. I know my theoretical preparation is weaker than my practical one, so I am reviewing probability, optimisation and the maths of learning. I think the combination of programming skills and real deployment experience is a good starting point for a taught programme.
**中文翻译**：我的学位是计算机科学，所以课程里有编程、数据结构、算法、线性代数和概率。对 AI，我训练过 YOLOv5 检测器，采集并标注了约 1800 张植物图像，量化为 INT8 部署到边缘芯片，并用 LangGraph 搭了决策链。我知道理论准备弱于实践，所以正在复习概率、优化和学习的数学。我认为编程能力加真实部署经验是授课型项目的一个好起点。
**追问 1**：How did you label the images? — With a labelling tool, in three classes: normal, wilting and disease spots. *（中文：怎么标注图像？— 用标注工具，三类：正常、萎蔫和病斑。）*
**追问 2**：How good was the detector? — mAP at 0.5 was about 0.86 in floating point and 0.83 in INT8. *（中文：检测器多好？— 浮点 mAP@0.5 约 0.86，INT8 约 0.83。）*
**关键词与易错点**：programming + deployment；数字取自 P4 自查表（约 1800 张、3 类）；承认理论弱。

### Q55. The PS mentions a robot learning project. What would it be?

**中文题意**：陈述里提到想做机器人学习项目。会是什么？
**考察点**：研究兴趣的具体度（项目未承诺机器人方向）。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I hope to do a project on learning-based perception or decision for a robot task, but I understand that the programme is a general AI programme and that a robotics project is not guaranteed. A realistic option is a perception project, for example detection or pose estimation for a robot manipulation or navigation task, with an evaluation on a small dataset or in simulation. I would define the metrics first, such as precision and recall and the speed on the target device, and compare with a baseline. I would also be happy with a non-robot AI project if it teaches me the same skills.
**中文翻译**：我希望做一个机器人任务的基于学习的感知或决策项目，但我理解这是通用 AI 项目，机器人项目并无保证。一个现实的选择是感知项目，例如机器人操作或导航任务的检测或位姿估计，在小数据集或仿真中评估。我会先定义指标，如精确率、召回率和目标设备上的速度，并与基线比较。如果非机器人的 AI 项目能教我同样的技能，我也乐意。
**追问 1**：What metric? — Precision, recall and frames per second on the device. *（中文：什么指标？— 精确率、召回率和设备上的帧率。）*
**追问 2**：What baseline? — A simple pretrained model or a rule-based approach. *（中文：什么基线？— 简单的预训练模型或基于规则的方法。）*
**关键词与易错点**：realistic、not guaranteed；不要承诺能做机器人项目。

### Q56. What do you expect from the practical side of the programme?

**中文题意**：你期待项目的实践部分是什么？
**考察点**：与培养目标“实践理解”的对应。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：From the aims of the programme, I understand that practice is part of the training: applying AI development tools and designing AI systems. I expect to work on projects where I build a complete pipeline: data, training, evaluation and deployment, probably in teams. This is close to how I learned: I built the full loop of my gardening system from the camera to the pump. I would like to bring engineering discipline: clear requirements, tests, and version control, and to learn from classmates with different strengths. I will check what facilities and platforms are available.
**中文翻译**：从项目目标，我理解实践是训练的一部分：应用 AI 开发工具和设计 AI 系统。我期待做完整流水线的项目：数据、训练、评估和部署，可能是团队合作。这接近我学习的方式：我从摄像头到水泵搭建了园艺系统的完整回路。我想带来工程纪律：清晰的需求、测试和版本控制，并向不同强项的同学学习。我会核对有哪些设施和平台可用。
**追问 1**：What is a complete pipeline? — Data collection, labelling, training, evaluation, deployment and monitoring. *（中文：什么是完整流水线？— 数据采集、标注、训练、评估、部署和监控。）*
**追问 2**：Teams or individual? — Both are fine. *（中文：团队还是个人？— 都可以。）*
**关键词与易错点**：full loop、engineering discipline；不要断言项目设施。

**B. 课程与研究方向（Q57–Q59）**

### Q57. How would you design a high-quality AI system? Use your gardening system as an example.

**中文题意**：你如何设计高质量的 AI 系统？以园艺系统为例。
**考察点**：对培养目标“设计高质量 AI 系统”的理解。
**English answer（约 110 词）**：For me, quality means correct, reliable, safe and maintainable. In the gardening system, I made a few design choices. The AI part is limited to observation and recommendation: the vision model gives detections with confidence and timestamps, and a decision chain gives structured recommendations. The physical action, watering, is controlled by a local state machine with start and stop thresholds, a maximum run time and a manual override. A fast rule path handles extreme states, such as very dry soil, without waiting for the model. Everything is logged in a traceable state. So AI adds value, while safety does not depend on it.
**中文翻译**：对我来说，质量意味着正确、可靠、安全和可维护。在园艺系统里，我做了几个设计选择。AI 部分限于观测和建议：视觉模型给出带置信度和时间戳的检测，决策链给出结构化建议。物理动作，也就是浇水，由本地状态机控制，带起停阈值、最长运行时间和手动覆盖。快速规则路径处理极端状态，如土壤过干，不必等待模型。一切都记录在可追溯的状态里。这样 AI 增加价值，而安全不依赖它。
**追问 1**：What is the maximum pump time? — About 60 seconds, then a forced stop and a cool-down. *（中文：水泵最长运行多久？— 约 60 秒，然后强制停止并冷却。）*
**追问 2**：What happens if the sensor fails? — The state becomes invalid, the pump stops, and a fault is reported. *（中文：传感器失效怎么办？— 状态置无效，关泵并上报故障。）*
**关键词与易错点**：AI limited to recommendation、local safety、fast path；数字取自 P4 自查表；不要说大模型控制水泵。

### Q58. What AI domain would you like to specialise in and why?

**中文题意**：你想专攻哪个 AI 领域，为什么？
**考察点**：对培养目标“识别特定 AI 领域关键问题”的回应。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I am most interested in perception and decision for physical systems. The key problems that I see in this domain are robustness outside the training conditions, such as lighting changes; efficiency on small devices; evaluation that reflects the real task; and safe execution of the decisions. I met the first two in my gardening project, where the detector needed data from different light conditions, and quantisation reduced accuracy a little. I would like to study these systematically, for example with better data, calibration and uncertainty estimates.
**中文翻译**：我最感兴趣的是物理系统的感知与决策。我在这个领域看到的关键问题有：训练条件之外的鲁棒性，如光照变化；小设备上的效率；反映真实任务的评估；以及决策的安全执行。前两项我在园艺项目里遇到过：检测器需要不同光照条件的数据，量化让精度略有下降。我想系统地研究这些，例如用更好的数据、校准和不确定性估计。
**追问 1**：What is uncertainty estimation? — Giving a model a measure of how sure it is. *（中文：什么是不确定性估计？— 让模型给出它有多确定的度量。）*
**追问 2**：How did you handle lighting? — I added data from day, cloudy, supplemental light and night. *（中文：怎么处理光照？— 加入白天、阴天、补光和夜间的数据。）*
**关键词与易错点**：robustness、efficiency、evaluation；不夸大研究；P4 光照数据是自查表取值。

### Q59. What is your view on responsible AI, and how would you apply it in a project?

**中文题意**：你怎么看负责任的 AI？项目里怎么落实？
**考察点**：AI 伦理与工程落地。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：Responsible AI means that a system is safe, fair, transparent and accountable. In a project, I would apply it in concrete steps. I would document the data and its limits, to find bias early. I would evaluate on different conditions and groups, not only on the average. I would add checks and limits around the model outputs, and keep logs so decisions can be audited.
