I would be honest about what the system cannot do, and avoid claiming more than the evidence supports. In my gardening project, I made the language model advisory only, which is a simple example.
**中文翻译**：负责任的 AI 意味着系统安全、公平、透明且可问责。在项目中，我会通过具体步骤落实。我会记录数据及其局限，以便早期发现偏见。我会在不同条件和群体上评估，而不只是看平均值。我会在模型输出周围加检查和限制，并保留日志以便审计。我会诚实说明系统不能做什么，不声称超出证据的东西。在我的园艺项目里，我让语言模型只做建议，这是一个简单的例子。
**追问 1**：What is bias in data? — A systematic difference between the data and the real situation or groups. *（中文：数据偏见是什么？— 数据与真实情形或群体之间的系统性差异。）*
**追问 2**：What does transparent mean? — People can understand how and why it made a decision. *（中文：透明指什么？— 人们能理解它如何、为何做出决策。）*
**关键词与易错点**：safe, fair, accountable；落到具体步骤；不空谈。

**C. 该校面试形式可能出现的题（Q60–Q63）**

### Q60. In 60 seconds: describe your AI-related experience.

**中文题意**：60 秒内讲你与 AI 相关的经验。
**考察点**：录制式视频面试的典型限时题。
**English answer（约 125 词）**：My AI experience is practical. In my gardening project, I collected and labelled about 1,800 plant images, trained a YOLOv5 model for three classes, and converted it to INT8 for an RK3588 chip, where it runs at about 15 frames per second. Then I built a decision chain with LangGraph that combines vision events and sensor data into recommendations. A local controller, not the language model, controls the pump. At Kaihong, I work on a chain that checks AI task plans before they become robot actions. So I know how to deploy a model and how to make AI decisions safe. What I need now is the theory, and that is why I am applying.
**中文翻译**：我的 AI 经验是实践性的。在园艺项目里，我采集并标注了约 1800 张植物图像，训练了三类 YOLOv5 模型，并转成 INT8 部署到 RK3588 芯片，约 15 帧每秒。然后我用 LangGraph 搭了决策链，把视觉事件和传感数据合成建议。控制水泵的是本地控制器，不是语言模型。在深开鸿，我做一条在 AI 任务计划变成机器人动作前检查它们的链条。所以我懂如何部署模型，也懂如何让 AI 决策安全。我现在需要的是理论，这就是我申请的原因。
**追问 1**：Which part took the most time? — Data collection and checking the quantised model. *（中文：哪部分最耗时？— 数据采集和检查量化后的模型。）*
**追问 2**：What would you do with more time? — Collect more varied data and test other model sizes. *（中文：时间更多会做什么？— 采集更多样的数据，测试其他模型规格。）*
**关键词与易错点**：practical、15 FPS、local controller；一句话结论放第一句；60 秒约 125 词。

### Q61. In 60 seconds: what AI application impressed you recently, and why?

**中文题意**：60 秒内：最近哪个 AI 应用让你印象深刻，为什么？
**考察点**：关注前沿，不编造具体新闻。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I will talk about a category and not a specific headline, since I want to be accurate. AI coding assistants impressed me, because they changed how quickly I can get from an idea to a working prototype. They are good at routine code and at explaining unfamiliar libraries. But I learned to check everything with tests, since generated code can be wrong in subtle ways, which matters for systems that control hardware. This shows me the pattern I believe in: AI is powerful as an assistant, and reliability comes from verification. I would like to understand how these models work, not only how to use them.
**中文翻译**：我讲一个类别而不是具体新闻，因为我想保持准确。AI 编程助手让我印象深刻，因为它们改变了我从想法到可运行原型的速度。它们擅长常规代码和解释陌生的库。但我也学会用测试检查一切，因为生成的代码可能有细微错误，这对控制硬件的系统很重要。这体现了我相信的模式：AI 作为助手很强大，可靠性来自验证。我想理解这些模型如何工作，而不只是如何使用。
**追问 1**：Do you use them in your projects? — As an assistant, with tests and review. *（中文：项目里用吗？— 作为助手，并有测试和评审。）*
**追问 2**：What worries you? — Over-trust without verification. *（中文：你担心什么？— 没有验证的过度信任。）*
**关键词与易错点**：category not headline、verification；不要编造具体产品发布；遵守课程的学术诚信规定。

### Q62. If the interview is recorded and has no follow-up, how do you make a short answer complete?

**中文题意**：如果面试是录制式、没有追问，如何让短回答完整？
**考察点**：对形式的适应（二手信息：录制式、限时、无追问）。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I use a fixed structure: first one sentence with my answer, then one concrete example with what I did and the result, and finally a short lesson or a link to the programme. Because nobody can ask me to clarify, I put the key evidence in the answer, for example a project name, my action and one number or outcome. I keep sentences short and the numbers few. I watch the time and start closing at about fifty seconds for a sixty-second question. I practise with a timer and a recording to check the rhythm.
**中文翻译**：我用固定的结构：先一句话给出回答，然后一个具体例子，写明我做了什么和结果，最后一句简短的收获或与项目的联系。因为没人能让我澄清，我把关键证据放进回答里，如项目名、我的动作和一个数字或结果。句子保持简短，数字不多。我留意时间，对六十秒的题在约五十秒开始收尾。我用计时器和录音练习，检查节奏。
**追问 1**：What if you run out of time mid-sentence? — The most important point is first, so little is lost. *（中文：如果句子中间时间到了？— 最重要的在前面，损失很小。）*
**追问 2**：How many numbers per answer? — One or two. *（中文：每个回答几个数字？— 一两个。）*
**关键词与易错点**：answer–example–lesson；见 [20 号文件](./20_题库_AI视频面试与限时作答.md)；以邀请邮件的说明为准。

### Q63. If this is a live interview instead, how would you handle follow-up questions about your project?

**中文题意**：如果是真人面试，你怎么应对项目追问？
**考察点**：三层深挖的准备。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：I prepare three levels for each project: what it is, how it works, and why I made each choice, including what went wrong. I answer the first question directly, and then add a detail only if asked. If I am asked something I do not remember exactly, I say so and give what I can reason, for example "I don't remember the exact number, but roughly …". I separate clearly what I did myself from what my team did. And if I do not understand the question, I ask the interviewer to rephrase it.
**中文翻译**：对每个项目我准备三层：它是什么、怎么工作、每个选择为什么这样，包括出了什么问题。我先直接回答第一问，被问到时再加细节。如果问到我记不准的内容，我会说明，并给出能推理出的部分，例如“具体数字我记不清，大概是……”。我清楚区分自己做的与团队做的。如果没听懂问题，我会请面试官换个说法。
**追问 1**：Which project is easiest for you to go deep? — The ballbot and the wheel-legged robot. *（中文：哪个项目最容易讲深？— 球形机器人和轮腿机器人。）*
**追问 2**：What do you do if you don’t know? — Say so, reason from principles, and ask whether I am on the right track. *（中文：不知道怎么办？— 说明，从原理推理，并确认方向对不对。）*
**关键词与易错点**：three levels、honest boundary；不要硬撑；不要编数字。

**D. 技术考点预测（Q64–Q73）**

### Q64. Explain the bias–variance trade-off.

**中文题意**：解释偏差–方差权衡。
**考察点**：ML 基础。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：Prediction error can be split into bias, variance and noise. Bias is the error from wrong assumptions, such as a model that is too simple, so it underfits. Variance is the error from sensitivity to the training data, such as a very flexible model, so it overfits. A simple model has high bias and low variance, a complex model the opposite. The goal is a balance that gives low error on new data. We find it using validation data, regularisation, more data, and by choosing the model size. For my detector, using a small model with limited data kept variance under control.
**中文翻译**：预测误差可分解为偏差、方差和噪声。偏差是错误假设造成的误差，如模型太简单，造成欠拟合。方差是对训练数据敏感造成的误差，如模型非常灵活，造成过拟合。简单模型偏差高方差低，复杂模型相反。目标是在新数据上获得低误差的平衡。我们用验证数据、正则化、更多数据和选择模型规模来找到它。对我的检测器，在数据有限时用小模型控制了方差。
**追问 1**：How do you see underfitting? — Both training and validation errors are high. *（中文：怎么看出欠拟合？— 训练和验证误差都高。）*
**追问 2**：Does more data reduce bias? — It mainly reduces variance. *（中文：更多数据降低偏差吗？— 主要降低方差。）*
**关键词与易错点**：bias、variance、underfit/overfit；不要说“more data always helps”。

### Q65. Explain L1 and L2 regularisation and dropout.

**中文题意**：解释 L1、L2 正则化和 dropout。
**考察点**：正则化。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：L2 regularisation adds the sum of squared weights to the loss. It shrinks weights smoothly toward zero and discourages large weights. L1 adds the sum of absolute values, which tends to produce sparse weights, with many exactly zero, so it works as a form of feature selection. Dropout randomly sets some neuron outputs to zero during training, so the network cannot rely on specific neurons, and it behaves like an average of many smaller networks. At test time, dropout is turned off. All three reduce overfitting by limiting the effective complexity of the model. I know them from courses and use them through frameworks.
**中文翻译**：L2 正则化把权重平方和加入损失，使权重平滑地向零收缩，抑制大权重。L1 加入绝对值之和，倾向于得到稀疏权重，许多恰为零，所以起到特征选择的作用。Dropout 在训练时随机把一些神经元输出置零，使网络不能依赖特定神经元，行为类似许多较小网络的平均。测试时关闭 dropout。三者都通过限制模型的有效复杂度来减少过拟合。我从课程中了解它们，并通过框架使用。
**追问 1**：Why does L1 give sparsity? — The absolute value has a corner at zero that pushes small weights exactly to zero. *（中文：L1 为什么稀疏？— 绝对值在零点有尖角，会把小权重推到恰好为零。）*
**追问 2**：Do you use dropout in YOLO? — I used the default training recipe and focused on data. *（中文：YOLO 里用 dropout 吗？— 我用了默认训练配置，重点放在数据上。）*
**关键词与易错点**：sparse、shrink、random off；不要说自己调过这些超参。

### Q66. Explain precision, recall, F1 and mAP. Why not just accuracy?

**中文题意**：解释精确率、召回率、F1 和 mAP。为什么不只看准确率？
**考察点**：评估指标（P4）。
**English answer（约 105 词）**：Precision is the fraction of detections that are correct. Recall is the fraction of real objects that were found. F1 is their harmonic mean. For detection, a prediction counts as correct if its overlap with the true box, the IoU, is above a threshold. Average precision is the area under the precision–recall curve for one class, and mAP is the mean over classes, possibly over several IoU thresholds. Accuracy can mislead with imbalanced classes: if most plants are healthy, a model that always says healthy has high accuracy. In my detector, wilting and disease spots were the important classes, so I looked at recall per class.
**中文翻译**：精确率是检测中正确的比例。召回率是真实物体中被找到的比例。F1 是两者的调和平均。对检测，如果预测与真实框的重叠（IoU）高于阈值，则算正确。平均精度是一个类别的精确率–召回率曲线下面积，mAP 是各类别的平均，可能还跨多个 IoU 阈值。类别不平衡时准确率会误导：如果大多数植物是健康的，一个总说“健康”的模型准确率就很高。在我的检测器里，萎蔫和病斑是重要类别，所以我看每类的召回率。
**追问 1**：What were your recalls? — Around 0.8 for wilting and about 0.73 for disease spots after INT8. *（中文：召回率是多少？— INT8 后萎蔫约 0.8，病斑约 0.73。）*
**追问 2**：What is IoU? — Intersection area divided by union area. *（中文：IoU 是什么？— 交集面积除以并集面积。）*
**关键词与易错点**：class imbalance、PR curve；数字取自 P4 自查表（wilting 0.80、disease_spot 0.73），说 about。

### Q67. How would you improve the detector if recall for disease spots is low?

**中文题意**：如果病斑召回率低，你怎么改进检测器？
**考察点**：问题诊断与数据思维。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：I would first look at the failure cases: are the missed spots small, in poor light, or confused with other textures? Based on that, I would collect more examples of those cases, because data is usually the first fix. I would check label quality, add augmentation such as brightness changes and scaling, and consider a larger input size or model if the speed allows. I would also check the quantisation: if the drop happens mostly after INT8, I would improve the calibration set or use a mixed precision for sensitive layers. And I would lower the confidence threshold carefully, because that raises recall but costs precision.
**中文翻译**：我会先看失败案例：漏检的病斑是小的、光线差的，还是与其他纹理混淆？据此我会采集更多这类样本，因为数据通常是第一选择。我会检查标注质量，增加数据增强，如亮度变化和缩放，并在速度允许时考虑更大的输入尺寸或模型。我也会检查量化：如果下降主要发生在 INT8 之后，我会改进校准集，或对敏感层用混合精度。我会谨慎降低置信度阈值，因为这提高召回但牺牲精确率。
**追问 1**：How many calibration images did you use? — About 200 images covering day, cloudy, supplemental light and night. *（中文：校准用多少张？— 约 200 张，覆盖白天、阴天、补光和夜间。）*
**追问 2**：Did you try mixed precision? — No, my model was fully INT8. *（中文：试过混合精度吗？— 没有，我的模型是全 INT8。）*
**关键词与易错点**：failure analysis、data first、threshold trade-off；诚实说没试混合精度。

### Q68. What is a convolutional neural network, and why is it suited to images?

**中文题意**：什么是卷积神经网络？为什么适合图像？
**考察点**：CNN 基础。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：A convolutional network applies small learnable filters across the image. Each filter detects a local pattern, such as an edge, and the same filter is used at every position, so the number of parameters is small and the network is translation-equivariant. Layers are stacked, so early layers find edges and textures, and later layers combine them into parts and objects. Pooling or strided convolutions reduce the size and give some invariance to small shifts. Compared with a fully connected network, a CNN uses the spatial structure of images and needs far fewer parameters. YOLO uses convolutional backbones for these reasons.
**中文翻译**：卷积网络在图像上应用小的可学习滤波器。每个滤波器检测局部模式，如边缘，同一滤波器用于每个位置，所以参数少，并具有平移等变性。层层堆叠，早期层找边缘和纹理，后期层把它们组合成部件和物体。池化或步长卷积减小尺寸，并带来对小位移的一定不变性。与全连接网络相比，CNN 利用图像的空间结构，需要的参数少得多。YOLO 出于这些原因使用卷积骨干。
**追问 1**：What is a receptive field? — The region of the input that affects one output unit. *（中文：什么是感受野？— 影响一个输出单元的输入区域。）*
**追问 2**：Why use pooling? — To reduce size and add a little shift invariance. *（中文：为什么用池化？— 减小尺寸并增加少量位移不变性。）*
**关键词与易错点**：shared filters、hierarchy；概念水平；不要说“CNN 无法处理其他数据”。

### Q69. Explain cross-entropy loss and why we use it for classification.

**中文题意**：解释交叉熵损失，为什么用于分类？
**考察点**：损失函数。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：For classification, the model outputs class probabilities, usually through softmax. Cross-entropy loss is the negative log of the probability assigned to the true class. If the model is confident and right, the loss is small; if it is confident and wrong, the loss is large. It is the negative log-likelihood of the labels under the model, so minimising it is maximum likelihood estimation. It also gives good gradients with softmax: the gradient with respect to the logits is simply the predicted probability minus the true label. Mean squared error works poorly here, since gradients vanish when the output saturates.
**中文翻译**：对分类，模型通常通过 softmax 输出类别概率。交叉熵损失是分配给真实类别的概率的负对数。模型自信且正确时损失小；自信但错误时损失大。它是标签在模型下的负对数似然，所以最小化它就是最大似然估计。与 softmax 配合还有良好的梯度：对 logits 的梯度就是预测概率减去真实标签。均方误差在此效果差，因为输出饱和时梯度会消失。
**追问 1**：What is softmax? — It turns scores into probabilities that sum to one. *（中文：softmax 是什么？— 把分数变成和为一的概率。）*
**追问 2**：What is a logit? — The raw score before softmax. *（中文：logit 是什么？— softmax 前的原始分数。）*
**关键词与易错点**：negative log-likelihood、softmax；公式要说清；不要把交叉熵和 MSE 混用。

### Q70. Compare SGD, momentum and Adam.

**中文题意**：比较 SGD、动量和 Adam。
**考察点**：优化器。
**English answer（约 95 词）**：Stochastic gradient descent updates the weights using the gradient of a mini-batch, with a learning rate. It is simple and often generalises well, but it can be slow and sensitive to the learning rate. Momentum adds a running average of past gradients, which smooths the updates and helps move through narrow valleys. Adam keeps running averages of the gradient and of its square, and scales the step for each parameter, so it adapts the learning rate and usually converges quickly with little tuning. In practice, many vision models are trained with SGD and momentum or Adam variants, and a learning-rate schedule matters a lot.
**中文翻译**：随机梯度下降用小批量的梯度和学习率更新权重。它简单且往往泛化好，但可能较慢，对学习率敏感。动量加入过去梯度的滑动平均，使更新平滑，有助于穿过狭窄山谷。Adam 保留梯度及其平方的滑动平均，并按参数缩放步长，因此自适应学习率，通常收敛快、调参少。实践中许多视觉模型用带动量的 SGD 或 Adam 变体训练，学习率调度很重要。
**追问 1**：What does the learning-rate schedule do? — It lowers the step size over time for fine convergence. *（中文：学习率调度做什么？— 随时间降低步长以精细收敛。）*
**追问 2**：Which did you use? — The default optimiser of the YOLOv5 training recipe. *（中文：你用哪个？— YOLOv5 训练配置的默认优化器。）*
**关键词与易错点**：mini-batch、momentum、adaptive；不要声称自己调过优化器。

### Q71. How does your LangGraph decision chain work, and how do you guard against wrong outputs from the language model?

**中文题意**：LangGraph 决策链如何工作？如何防范语言模型的错误输出？
**考察点**：LLM Agent 工程（P4）。
**English answer（约 110 词）**：The chain is a graph of steps. It takes the latest vision event and the sensor state, checks that they are fresh, runs a fast rule path for extreme cases, and otherwise calls the language model with a prompt that asks for a JSON answer with a fixed schema, at low temperature. The output is validated against the schema. Any command request goes through a whitelist and policy check before it reaches the device layer, and the device controller has its own limits. If the model times out or returns invalid output, the system falls back to doing nothing or to an alert. So the model can advise, but cannot act directly.
**中文翻译**：决策链是一个步骤图。它取最新的视觉事件和传感状态，检查是否新鲜，对极端情形走快速规则路径，否则用要求固定 schema 的 JSON 回答的提示、以低温度调用语言模型。输出要按 schema 校验。任何命令请求在到达设备层之前要通过白名单和策略检查，设备控制器自己也有限制。如果模型超时或返回无效输出，系统回退到不动作或告警。所以模型可以建议，但不能直接行动。
**追问 1**：What temperature did you use? — Low, about 0.2, for stable outputs. *（中文：温度设多少？— 低，约 0.2，以保证输出稳定。）*
**追问 2**：What timeout? — About ten seconds for the model call. *（中文：超时多少？— 模型调用约十秒。）*
**关键词与易错点**：schema validation、whitelist、fallback；取值取自 P4 自查表；模型是 DeepSeek-V3 类通用对话模型，面试中说 “a general chat model through an OpenAI-compatible API”。

### Q72. Did you use retrieval-augmented generation in your project? How does RAG work?

**中文题意**：你的项目用了检索增强生成吗？RAG 如何工作？
**考察点**：诚实地区分“实现了什么”和“概念”。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：In my project, I did not implement a vector-based RAG. I kept a short-term memory of recent event summaries, and a simple keyword fallback over about twenty short care-knowledge texts, returning the top three matches. About RAG in general: documents are split into chunks and embedded as vectors and stored in an index. At query time, the question is embedded, the nearest chunks are retrieved, and they are added to the prompt, so the model can answer using the retrieved text. It reduces hallucination and allows updating knowledge without retraining, but quality depends on chunking, retrieval and the prompt.
**中文翻译**：我的项目里没有实现基于向量的 RAG。我保留了最近事件摘要的短期记忆，以及对约二十条简短护理知识文本的关键词降级检索，返回前三条匹配。关于一般的 RAG：文档被切成片段并嵌入为向量，存入索引。查询时，把问题嵌入，检索最近的片段并加入提示，使模型能依据检索到的文本回答。它减少幻觉，并允许无需重训就更新知识，但质量取决于切分、检索和提示。
**追问 1**：What is an embedding? — A vector representation where similar meanings are close. *（中文：什么是嵌入？— 相近含义的向量表示距离近。）*
**追问 2**：How would you add vector RAG? — Embed the care texts, store them in a vector index, and retrieve by similarity. *（中文：怎么加向量 RAG？— 嵌入护理文本，存入向量索引，按相似度检索。）*
**关键词与易错点**：honest about what was implemented、chunk/embed/retrieve；P4：长期记忆/向量 RAG 未实现；不要说做了 RAG。

### Q73. What are the main limitations of large language models, and how would you design around them?

**中文题意**：大语言模型的主要局限是什么？如何围绕它们设计？
**考察点**：LLM 理解与工程对策。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：Main limitations are hallucination, where the model states false things confidently; sensitivity to the prompt; limited and sometimes outdated knowledge; latency and cost; and difficulty of verifying outputs. To design around them, I would constrain the output format with schemas, ground the answer on retrieved or provided data, validate the output with rules and checks, keep the model out of safety-critical paths, add timeouts and fallbacks, and log inputs and outputs for audit. For evaluation, I would build a small test set of realistic cases and measure the failure modes, and not just trust a few good examples.
**中文翻译**：主要局限是幻觉，即模型自信地陈述错误内容；对提示敏感；知识有限且有时过时；延迟和成本；以及难以验证输出。围绕它们设计时，我会用 schema 约束输出格式，让回答基于检索或提供的数据，用规则和检查验证输出，把模型排除在安全关键路径之外，加超时和回退，并记录输入输出以便审计。评估时，我会构建一个贴近实际情形的小测试集并衡量失败模式，而不是只相信几个好例子。
**追问 1**：What is hallucination? — Plausible but false content produced by the model. *（中文：什么是幻觉？— 模型产生的看似合理但错误的内容。）*
**追问 2**：What is grounding? — Basing the answer on given evidence. *（中文：什么是“落地依据”（grounding）？— 让回答基于给定证据。）*
**关键词与易错点**：hallucination、schema、fallback、audit；见 [14 题库](./14_题库_机器学习_深度学习_强化学习_大模型.md)；不绝对化。

**E. 项目深挖与收尾（Q74–Q75）**

### Q74. Explain reinforcement learning: states, actions, rewards, policy and value.

**中文题意**：解释强化学习：状态、动作、奖励、策略和价值。
**考察点**：AI 项目的基础概念；你只有概念水平。
**English answer（约 100 词）**：In reinforcement learning, an agent interacts with an environment. At each step it observes a state, takes an action, receives a reward and moves to a new state. The policy is the rule that maps states to actions. The return is the discounted sum of future rewards, and the value of a state is the expected return from that state under the policy. The goal is to find a policy that maximises the expected return. Methods include value-based ones, like Q-learning, and policy-gradient ones. The exploration–exploitation trade-off is key. I know it at the concept level and want to practise it in the programme.
**中文翻译**：在强化学习中，智能体与环境交互。每一步它观察状态、采取动作、获得奖励并转到新状态。策略是把状态映射到动作的规则。回报是未来奖励的折扣和，状态的价值是该策略下从该状态出发的期望回报。目标是找到使期望回报最大的策略。方法包括基于价值的，如 Q 学习，以及策略梯度类。探索与利用的权衡是关键。我在概念层面了解，想在项目中练习。
**追问 1**：What is the discount factor for? — To weigh near rewards more than distant ones. *（中文：折扣因子的作用？— 让近期奖励权重大于远期奖励。）*
**追问 2**：Have you trained an agent? — No. *（中文：训练过智能体吗？— 没有。）*
**关键词与易错点**：state–action–reward、return、exploration；诚实说明概念水平。

### Q75. What questions would you like to ask us?

**中文题意**：你有什么问题想问我们？
**考察点**：反问质量。
**English answer（约 90 词）**：I have three questions. First, how is the final project organised in the programme, and is there a possibility to work on robotics or embodied AI applications? Second, what computing resources, such as GPUs or lab platforms, can students use for projects? Third, how does the programme support students in finding internships or industry projects in the Greater Bay Area? I have read the programme page and the aims, and I would like to understand how they are carried out in practice. Thank you for your time.
**中文翻译**：我有三个问题。第一，项目中的最终项目是如何组织的，是否有可能做机器人或具身 AI 应用？第二，学生做项目时可以使用哪些计算资源，如 GPU 或实验室平台？第三，项目如何支持学生在大湾区找实习或产业项目？我已读过项目页面和培养目标，想了解它们在实践中如何落实。感谢您的时间。
**追问 1**：Why ask about robotics? — Because that is my application domain. *（中文：为什么问机器人？— 因为那是我的应用领域。）*
**追问 2**：Any question about accommodation? — I would check the official information. *（中文：关于住宿？— 我会看官方信息。）*
**关键词与易错点**：project、compute、industry links；不问官网已写的信息。
